*Seung Hwan Cho,Young-Min Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了多任务学习（MTL）在第二语言语音识别中的一个关键问题：共享表示并非对"发音转录"和"语义理解"两个任务都有益。研究发现，MTL虽然提升了语义任务，却损害了表面转录任务（尤其在英语中），其退化程度与表面-语义的编辑距离分歧正相关。编码器分析表明，根本原因在于编码器层面的任务表示纠缠：韩语能保持任务表示的区分性，而英语中则趋于同质化。解码器分析进一步显示，语义解码器能自适应形成独特表示，而表面解码器则受编码器限制，无法解脱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究普遍假设多任务学习中共享表示对发音转录和语义理解两个输出均有促进作用，但该假设在L2语音识别中（特别是跨韩语和英语场景下）是否成立未经严格检验。作者试图验证并挑战这一假设。

Method: 1. 构建双输出L2 ASR的多任务学习框架，同时预测发音（surface transcription）和语义（meaning）；2. 以Levenshtein编辑距离量化表面-语义分歧度；3. 通过编码器分析（encoder analysis）考察任务表示的纠缠程度；4. 通过跨任务解码器分析（cross-task decoder analysis）对比语义解码器与表面解码器的表示适应能力。

Result: 1. MTL提升语义识别但损害表面转录，英语中退化更严重；2. 表面退化程度与Levenshtein编辑距离正相关；3. 编码器层面：韩语保持两个任务的表征分离，英语中则几乎完全纠缠；4. 解码器层面：语义双输出解码器能形成独特表征进行适应，而表面解码器仍受制于编码器的纠缠表征。

Conclusion: MTL在L2 ASR中并非普遍有益，共享表示假设因语言而异。核心瓶颈在于编码器层面的任务表示纠缠。未来应设计能缓解编码器纠缠的MTL框架，以减轻双输出L2语音识别中的表面转录退化问题。

Abstract: Second-language (L2) speech recognition often requires transcriptions of pronunciations and intended meanings. Multi-task learning (MTL) is a natural approach because it assumes that shared representations benefit both outputs. However, this paper shows that this assumption does not hold across Korean and English. MTL improves meaning but degrades surface transcription, especially in English, where the degradation scales with surface-meaning divergence measured by Levenshtein edit distance.Encoder analysis links these patterns to encoder-level entanglement, with Korean preserving distinct task representations while English produces nearly identical ones. Cross-task decoder analysis shows that the meaning dual-output decoder adapts with a unique representation, while the surface dual-output decoder remains constrained by the encoder. These findings motivate the design of MTL frameworks that mitigate encoder-level entanglement to reduce surface degradation in dual-output L2 automatic speech recognition.

</details>


### [177] [SkillComposer: Learning to Evolve Agent Skills for Specification and Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06079)
*Qi Zhang,Zhaopeng Feng,Xiaonan Shi,Xiaomeng Hu,Chu Liu,Pengjun Xie,Xiaobin Wang,Jieping Ye,Bryan Hooi,Haobo Wang,Junbo Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SkillComposer 将技能构建分解为创建、改进和合并三个可学习操作，通过拒绝采样训练，使语言模型能在推理时自我进化技能，在多个基准上一致超越基线，并展现出跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前技能构建方法将其视为一次性提取，存在根本性张力：针对特定任务定制的技能难以迁移，而抽象技能又往往缺乏足够指导。缺乏显式的技能规范与泛化机制是导致这一脆弱性的根源。

Method: 提出 SkillComposer 框架，将技能构建分解为 create（创建）、improve（改进）、merge（合并）三个可学习操作。通过系统化的拒绝采样（rejection sampling）方法训练，支持三种部署模式：离线（构建通用技能库）、在线（任务特定优化）和混合模式。实验中将 SkillComposer-4B 与 27B 执行器模型配合使用。

Result: 在 τ²-Bench、LiveCodeBench v6 和 AppWorld 上，SkillComposer 一致超越基线：代理任务提升最高 +4.5，代码任务提升 +3.4。分析表明 merge 和 improve 解决正交的质量维度，技能组合是一种可迁移的元能力。

Conclusion: SkillComposer 为技能增强推理提供了实用方案，其技能组合能力可跨域泛化到未见过的任务类型，验证了将技能构建作为可学习组合过程的可行性。

Abstract: Agent skills, which consist of reusable strategies that guide agent reasoning and action, have shown strong potential for improving model capability at inference time. However, current skill construction methods treat the problem as one-shot extraction, overlooking a fundamental tension: a skill tailored to the specific task fails to transfer, while the abstracted skill often provides insufficient guidance. We attribute this fragility to the absence of explicit mechanisms for skill specification and generalization. To address this gap, we introduce SkillComposer, a framework that decomposes skill construction into three learnable operations: create, improve, and merge. Trained via systematic rejection sampling recipe, SkillComposer enables language models to self-evolve skills at inference time and supports three deployment modes: offline for building generalized libraries, online for task-specific refinement, and hybrid for combining both. Comprehensive experiments on $τ^2$-Bench, LiveCodeBench v6, and AppWorld show that SkillComposer consistently outperforms baselines. Our SkillComposer-4B improves a 27B executor by up to +4.5 on agent tasks and +3.4 on code tasks, while generalizing across domains and task types unseen during training. Analysis reveals that merge and improve address orthogonal quality dimensions and that skill composition is a transferable meta-ability, providing a practical recipe for skill-augmented inference.

</details>


### [178] [LatentSkill: From In-Context Textual Skills to In-Weight Latent Skills for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06087)
*Aofan Yu,Chenyu Zhou,Tianyi Xu,Zihan Guo,Rong Shan,Zhihui Fu,Jun Wang,Weiwen Liu,Yong Yu,Weinan Zhang,Jianghao Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LatentSkill 提出将文本技能通过预训练超网络转换为即插即用的 LoRA 适配器，将技能知识存储在权重空间而非上下文空间中，从而消除每步注入技能 token 的开销，同时保持模块化加载、缩放和组合能力，在 ALFWorld 和 Search-QA 上均以更少的 prefill token 超越了上下文内技能基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体系统普遍使用文本技能编码可复用任务流程，但每步向提示中注入技能会带来大量上下文开销，并暴露技能内容为明文。因此需要一种将技能从上下文空间迁移到权重空间的方案，以降低开销并增强安全性。

Method: 提出 LatentSkill 框架：(1) 通过预训练的超网络（hypernetwork）将文本技能转换为 LoRA 适配器，实现技能在权重空间中的存储；(2) 支持 LoRA 缩放系数对技能的精确控制；(3) 在技能组件对齐时，通过参数空间算术实现技能组合。

Result: 在 ALFWorld 上，LatentSkill 在 seen 和 unseen 划分上分别提升 21.4 和 13.4 个成功率点，同时减少 64.1% 的 prefill token；在 Search-QA 上，精确匹配提升 3.0 个点，技能 token 开销降低 72.2%。进一步分析表明，生成的技能 LoRA 形成结构化的语义几何空间。

Conclusion: 权重空间技能为扩展 LLM 智能体提供了一种高效、模块化且暴露程度更低的方案，推测未来可在此方向上进一步探索技能存储与组合机制。

Abstract: Agent systems increasingly use textual skills to encode reusable task procedures, but injecting these skills into the prompt at every step incurs substantial context overhead and exposes skill content as plaintext. We present LatentSkill, a framework that converts textual skills into plug-and-play LoRA adapters through a pretrained hypernetwork. LatentSkill stores skill knowledge in weight space rather than context space, removing per-step skill tokens while preserving modular loading, scaling, and composition. On ALFWorld and Search-QA, LatentSkill outperforms the corresponding in-context skill baseline while using substantially fewer prefill tokens: it improves ALFWorld success by 21.4 and 13.4 points on the seen and unseen splits with 64.1% fewer prefill tokens, and improves Search-QA exact match by 3.0 points with 72.2% lower skill-token overhead. Further analysis shows that generated skill LoRAs form a structured semantic geometry, can be precisely controlled via the LoRA scaling coefficient, and can be composed through parameter-space arithmetic when skill components are aligned. These findings suggest that weight-space skills provide an efficient, modular, and less exposed substrate for extending LLM agents.

</details>


### [179] [CHALIS: A Challenge Dataset for Language Identification in Difficult Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06088)
*Michal Tichý,Jindřich Libovický*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CHALIS是一个针对语言识别中困难场景的基准数据集，重点考察近亲语言和正字法噪声两种情况。数据集包含互懂语言对（如捷克语/斯洛伐克语）的共享句子，以及多种正字法扰动（转写、去变音符、同形字攻击、网络俚语）。评估表明现有语言识别系统在上述场景中表现显著下降，尤其在低资源近亲语言和转写文本上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言识别系统在近亲语言（cousin languages）区分和存在正字法噪声（orthographic noise）的输入上表现不佳，但缺乏专门针对这类困难场景的系统性基准。作者旨在填补这一空白，推动更鲁棒的语言识别技术发展。

Method: 构建CHALIS数据集，分为两部分：(1) 近亲语言对：收集捷克语/斯洛伐克语、西班牙语/加泰罗尼亚语、葡萄牙语/加利西亚语、丹麦语/挪威语四组互懂语言对的共享句子；(2) 正字法噪声：对文本进行多脚本转写、去除变音符、模拟同形字攻击（homoglyph attacks）、使用网络俚语。随后在四个广泛使用的语言识别系统上进行评估。

Result: 四个语言识别系统在CHALIS上均大幅受挫，尤其表现在：近亲语言对中的低资源语言识别困难，以及转写输入上的性能骤降。

Conclusion: 现有语言识别系统在面对近亲语言和正字法扰动时鲁棒性不足，CHALIS可作为针对性基准促进该领域改进。数据集已在Hugging Face上公开。

Abstract: We present CHALIS (Challenging Language Identification Samples), a new benchmark dataset explicitly designed to address difficult cases in language identification: cousin languages and orthographic noise. Our dataset has two parts: First, we collected sentences shared across mutually intelligible language pairs (Czech/Slovak, Spanish/Catalan, Portuguese/Galician, Danish/Norwegian). The second part tests for orthography noise: we transliterate text across multiple scripts, remove diacritics, simulate homoglyph attacks, and use Internet slang. We evaluate four widely used language identification systems on CHALIS and demonstrate that all struggle substantially in these scenarios, especially on lower-resource languages within cousin pairs and on transliterated input. The resource is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/michal-tichy/CHALIS.

</details>


### [180] [IR3DE: A Linear Router for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06098)
*Eros Fanì,Oğuzhan Ersoy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 IR3DE，一种基于岭回归的轻量路由方法，用于在多个领域专家 LLM 间快速选择最合适的模型，无需重新训练即可动态增减专家模型，在因果语言建模和推理任务上均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理路由方法要么仅在强弱通用 LLM 之间优化成本，要么需要大量训练来支持领域专家路由。缺少一种廉价、快速且能灵活支持动态增减领域专家的路由方案。

Method: 提出 IR3DE（Ridge Regression-based Router for Domain Experts），利用岭回归作为路由决策机制，为每个 prompt 提供低成本、快速的路由判断。支持在不重新训练的情况下动态添加或移除领域专家。

Result: 在两种因果语言建模（CLM）设置和一种推理设置下评估：CLM 场景中性能与其他基线相当，推理场景中超越基线，达到 98.4% 的归一化性能。

Conclusion: IR3DE 作为一种线性路由器，以极低的计算和训练成本实现了与复杂方法竞争甚至更优的路由性能，且具备灵活的专家动态管理能力，具有较好的实用价值。

Abstract: Foundational Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate proficiency on a wide range of general tasks, and achieve remarkable results on various specialized tasks via domain-expert LLMs. With the ever-growing list of available LLMs, inference routers are being proposed to select the most appropriate LLM for each prompt. However, existing routing methods either optimize cost across weak-to-strong generalist LLMs or require substantial training to support domain-expertise routing. In this paper, we propose IR3DE, a Ridge Regression-based Router for Domain Experts that provides cheap and fast routing decisions for each prompt. We evaluate IR3DE in two Causal Language Modeling (CLM) settings where the tasks are next-token prediction for all domains, and one reasoning setting where each domain has its own distinct reasoning task. Despite being a linear router, IR3DE achieves performance comparable to the other baselines in both CLM settings, and surpassing them in the reasoning setting, with a normalized performance of 98.4%. Moreover, IR3DE enables the addition or removal of new domain experts without requiring the router to be retrained from scratch, allowing a dynamic set of LLMs to be served with minimal disruption to the router itself. Our code is available at: github.com/gensyn-ai/IR3DE.

</details>


### [181] [Harnessing Structural Context for Entity Alignment Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06109)
*Xingyu Chen,Yuanning Cui,Zequn Sun,Wei Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ContextEA 提出一种增强的编码器-解码器框架，通过在编码端引入跨知识图谱交互编码器、在解码端引入结构校准解码器，显式利用结构上下文，显著提升了可迁移实体对齐基础模型在未见知识图谱对上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实体对齐基础模型存在两处结构上下文利用不足的问题：(1) 编码阶段跨知识图谱交互较弱；(2) 解码阶段候选排序过度依赖粗粒度相似度。这限制了模型向未见知识图谱的迁移能力。

Method: 提出 ContextEA 框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 跨知识图谱交互编码器——通过锚点桥接统一两个知识图谱，并进行早期关系感知的跨图传播；(2) 结构校准解码器——利用实体级、邻域级、关系级和锚点感知的结构证据来校准对齐分数。整体设计保持轻量化。

Result: 在 OpenEA、SRPRS 和 DBP 三个基准的 29 个 EA 数据集上进行实验，相比强可迁移基线取得一致提升。预训练的 ContextEA 在所有三个基准组上已超越微调后的基线模型。

Conclusion: 显式利用结构上下文是改进实体对齐基础模型的有效方向，ContextEA 展示了更强的向未见知识图谱的迁移能力。

Abstract: Entity alignment (EA) aims to identify equivalent entities across heterogeneous knowledge graphs (KGs) and is a key component of knowledge fusion and cross-KG reasoning. The recent EA foundation model demonstrates that alignment knowledge, once pretrained, can be directly applied to diverse previously unseen KG pairs. However, it still underuses structural context in two places: cross-KG interaction is weak during encoding, and final candidate ranking still relies too heavily on coarse similarity. We address these limitations with ContextEA, an enhanced encoder-decoder framework for transferable EA. On the encoder side, we introduce a cross-KG interaction encoder that unifies the two KGs with anchor bridges and performs earlier relation-aware cross-graph propagation. On the decoder side, we introduce a structural calibration decoder that calibrates alignment scores with entity-level, neighborhood-level, relation-level, and anchor-aware structural evidence. This design strengthens both structural context construction and structural context exploitation while remaining lightweight. Experiments on 29 EA datasets in OpenEA, SRPRS, and DBP show consistent gains over strong transferable baselines. Notably, the pretrained ContextEA already surpasses the finetuned baselines on all three benchmark groups, demonstrating substantially stronger transfer to unseen KGs. These results suggest that explicitly harnessing structural context is an effective direction for improving EA foundation models.

</details>


### [182] [Ouvia: A User-centered Framework for Measuring Usability of Speech Translation in Real-World Communication Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06177)
*Giuseppe Attanasio,Beatrice Savoldi,Daniel Chechelnitsky,Matteo Negri,Marine Carpuat,Maarten Sap,André F. T. Martins*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Ouvia 是一个面向真实场景中语音翻译可用性评估的框架，研究发现现代ST系统仅能满足约一半的交互需求，且不同人群间存在显著可用性差距；QA-based评估比传统指标更能预测真实可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语音翻译评估过度依赖去语境化的测试集和整体质量分数，忽视了终端用户的实际沟通需求，缺乏面向真实场景、以用户为中心的可用性评估框架。

Method: 开发 Ouvia 评估框架，面向一对一跨语言沟通场景（英语→葡萄牙语）；通过定制化网页应用和多阶段研究设计，在医疗和日常情境中收集超过1750次交互数据，涵盖四种ST系统、三种英语方言和两种性别的说话人。

Result: 现代ST系统仅约一半交互被评为"可用"；不同人口群体间可用性报告存在显著差距；在各类质量指标中，基于QA的评估方法对真实世界可用性的预测力显著优于传统评估方法。

Conclusion: 语音翻译评估应转向情境化、以用户为中心的框架，超越整体质量分数，关注技术服务于谁、服务效果如何。QA-based评估可作为更有效的可用性预测指标。

Abstract: Speech translation (ST) is increasingly adopted in user applications, yet its evaluation largely focuses on decontextualized testbeds and holistic quality, rather than end users' communication needs. We introduce Ouvia, an evaluation framework for measuring user-perceived usability of speech translation outputs in real-world settings. Ouvia focuses on one-to-one communication: an English speaker needs to convey a request to a Portuguese speaker, and the message is automatically translated. Through a custom web app and multi-phase study design, we collect more than 1,750 such interactions in healthcare and everyday situations, mediated by four ST systems, involving speakers from three English dialects and two genders. We find that modern ST serves people only to a limited extent -- only around half of interactions are rated as usable -- with significant gaps in reported usability across demographic groups. Moreover, among quality metrics, we find that QA-based evaluation is a substantially stronger predictor of real-world usability than standard approaches. Together, these findings stress the importance of situated, user-centered evaluation frameworks that go beyond holistic quality scores and attend to who the technology serves -- and how well.

</details>


### [183] [The Tell-Tale Norm: $\ell_2$ Magnitude as a Signal for Reasoning Dynamics in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06188)
*Jinyang Zhang,Hongxin Ding,Yue Fang,Weibin Liao,Muyang Ye,Junfeng Zhao,Yasha Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM隐藏状态的L2范数可作为模型内在推理强度的可靠信号，理论上证明了L2范数约束SAE推理特征的激活强度，并据此提出三种无需额外训练的测试时推理增强技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对LLM推理过程的理解缺乏一个原则性的、模型内在的信号来刻画层级推理动态，亟需找到一种能够忠实反映推理强度的内生指标。

Method: 以稀疏自编码器（SAE）作为诊断探针，观察推理特征的层级激活模式；建立隐藏状态L2范数与SAE推理特征激活强度的形式化理论联系；通过相关性分析和因果干预验证L2范数的指示作用；提出三种基于L2范数的测试时扩展技术：自适应层级推理递归、内生推理状态引导、L2引导的响应选择。

Result: LLM内部推理表现为后期层中推理特征激活的急剧上升；理论上证明了L2范数是SAE推理特征激活强度的上界；实证表明L2范数峰值与关键推理步骤高度一致；三种L2引导技术在多个模型架构和基准上显著提升了推理性能。

Conclusion: 隐藏状态的L2范数是一种原则性、简单且强大的内在信号，可用于感知和控制LLM的潜在推理动态，为理解和增强LLM推理能力提供了新的视角和实用工具。

Abstract: Recent work has sought to understand Large Language Models (LLMs) reasoning, yet a principled, model-intrinsic signal that captures its layer-wise reasoning dynamics remains underexplored. We bridge this gap by demonstrating that the l2 norm of hidden states serves as an endogenous signal of the model's reasoning intensity. Using Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) as a diagnostic probe, we observe that LLMs' internal reasoning is marked by a sharp increase in reasoning feature activations concentrated in late layers. Motivated by this pattern, we establish a formal link between reasoning intensity and the model's latent geometry and theoretically prove that the l2 norm of hidden states bounds the activation strength of SAE reasoning features. Empirical correlation analysis and causal interventions further validate the l2 norm as a faithful indicator, where heightened norms consistently correspond to critical reasoning steps. We then introduce three test-time scaling techniques guided by l2 norms: (i) Adaptive Layer-wise Reasoning Recursion, (ii) Endogenous Reasoning State Steering, and (iii) l2-guided Response Selection, which requires no additional training or data and is compatible with advanced inference engines. Experiments across model architectures and benchmarks show that l2-norm-based techniques significantly improve reasoning performance, offering a principled yet simple lens to perceive and control LLM latent reasoning dynamics. Our code is available at https://github.com/zjy1298/The-Tell-Tale-Norm.

</details>


### [184] [Improving Answer Extraction in Context-based Question Answering Systems Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06197)
*Hafez Abdelghaffar,Ahmed Alansary,Ali Hamdi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于大语言模型的问答系统，通过在SQuAD1.1数据集上微调预训练模型来改善上下文理解与答案提取能力。微调后的RoBERTa-base取得最佳效果：ROUGE-L 86.84%、BLEU 28.24%、BERTScore 95.38%，验证了针对性微调对问答系统可靠性和精度的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于大语言模型的问答系统虽然在整体上取得显著进展，但在面对复杂或模糊查询时，仍存在上下文理解不足、答案一致性差、跨领域泛化能力弱等问题，常常产生不相关或不精确的回答。该研究旨在解决这些局限性，提升系统从给定上下文中精确提取和生成答案的能力。

Method: 采用基于预训练大语言模型的监督微调方法。具体而言，使用斯坦福问答数据集（SQuAD1.1）提供的高质量"上下文-问题-答案"三元组，对预训练LLM进行微调，以增强其上下文理解与答案抽取能力。模型以文本上下文和对应问题为输入，输出简洁准确的答案。

Result: 微调后的RoBERTa-base模型在SQuAD1.1上取得最优性能：ROUGE-L分数达86.84%，BLEU分数达28.24%，BERTScore达95.38%。三项指标均表明模型在答案准确性和相关性方面表现突出。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，针对特定任务进行微调能够显著提升问答系统的可靠性（BERTScore 95.38%）和精确度（ROUGE-L 86.84%）。该工作验证了基于预训练LLM的监督微调策略在上下文问答任务中的有效性，为构建更精准的问答系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Question answering (QA) systems have achieved notable progress with the advent of large language models (LLMs). However, they still face challenges in accurately extracting and generating precise answers from given contexts, particularly when dealing with complex or ambiguous queries. Existing approaches often struggle with contextual understanding, answer consistency, and generalization across diverse domains. In this work, we propose a question answering system based on large language models, where the input consists of a textual context and a corresponding question, and the output is a concise and accurate answer. The motivation behind this research lies in addressing the limitations of current QA systems, particularly their tendency to produce irrelevant or imprecise responses despite having access to the correct context. Our methodology involves fine-tuning a pre-trained LLM on a benchmark QA dataset to improve its contextual comprehension and answer extraction capabilities. Specifically, we utilize the Stanford Question Answering Dataset (SQuAD1.1), which provides high-quality context-question-answer triplets for supervised training and evaluation. Experimental results show that the fine-tuned Roberta-base model achieves the highest performance, attaining a ROUGE-L score of 86.84%, a BLEU score of 28.24%, and a BERTScore of 95.38%. These results indicate strong accuracy and answer relevance, demonstrating the effectiveness of the proposed approach for context-based question answering tasks. Furthermore, the findings confirm that targeted fine-tuning substantially improves the reliability and precision of QA systems.

</details>


### [185] [Dense Contexts Are Hard Contexts: Lexical Density Limits Effective Context in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06203)
*Giovanni Dettori,Matteo Boffa,Danilo Giordano,Idilio Drago,Marco Mellia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 除了输入长度和相关信息位置，词汇密度（lexical density）——即上下文中引入新信息的速度——是系统性地缩减LLM有效上下文窗口的第三个关键因素。实验表明，在相同长度和针位置下，高密度基准导致模型检索性能急剧下降，从稀疏上下文的近乎完美跌至60%以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM长上下文性能退化的原因通常被归因于输入长度和相关信息位置，但词汇密度作为一个潜在的重要因素长期被忽视。研究者旨在揭示并量化词汇密度对有效上下文窗口的独立影响，排除长度和位置的混淆效应。

Method: 使用三个"大海捞针"（find-the-needle）风格的基准测试，所有基准具有相同的输入长度（约12k tokens）和受控的针位置，但信息密度递增。在9B到685B参数规模的开源LLM上进行评估。进一步在每个基准内部变化和控制密度，保持其他属性不变，以排除任务类型的混淆。

Result: 在低密度（稀疏）上下文中，模型表现近乎完美；但在高密度基准中，检索得分急剧下降至60%以下。通过降低高密度基准中的密度，性能普遍恢复，表明性能崩溃确实由词汇密度驱动，而非其他混淆变量。

Conclusion: 有效上下文容量是词汇密度的函数。对于处理紧凑、信息密集型输入的实际LLM系统而言，词汇密度是影响长上下文性能的关键因素，在设计和使用长上下文模型时必须予以考虑。

Abstract: Input length and the position of relevant information are widely cited as the primary causes of degraded LLM long-context performance. Here, we study lexical density -- the rate at which a context introduces distinct information -- as a third, largely overlooked factor that systematically reduces the effective context window of LLMs. We quantify the impact of lexical density on open-weight LLMs (9B-685B) using three "find-the-needle" style benchmarks with identical length (~12k tokens) and controlled needle position, but increasing density of information. We observe a sharp performance collapse in higher-density benchmarks: models that are near-perfect in sparse contexts drop below 60% retrieval score on denser ones. To rule out task-type confounds, we vary and control the density within each benchmark while keeping all other properties unchanged. Reducing density generally restores performance, especially in the high-density regimes where degradation appears. These results show that effective context capacity is a function of lexical density, with direct implications for real-world LLM systems operating on compact, information-rich inputs.

</details>


### [186] [FiLM-Based Speaker Conditioning of a SpeechLLM for Pathological Speech Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06211)
*Fernando López,Santosh Kesiraju,Jordi Luque*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究利用特征线性调制（FiLM）进行说话人条件化，将x-vector信息注入冻结的ASR编码器的每个Transformer层，以适应神经系统疾病导致的病理语音，无需修改基础模型权重。在西班牙语和英语病理语音上的实验表明，该方法与标准微调及参数高效微调基线相比具有竞争力，且能保留对非条件化语音的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管自动语音识别在标准语音上取得了显著进展，但神经系统疾病（如构音障碍等）导致的病理语音仍是一个重大挑战。病理语音具有高度变异性，且数据稀缺，直接微调大规模ASR模型容易过拟合。因此，需要探索一种既能适应个体说话人病理特征、又不需要修改预训练模型权重的高效自适应方法。

Method: 采用特征线性调制（Feature-wise Linear Modulation, FiLM）作为说话人条件化机制：从说话人音频中提取x-vector嵌入，将其通过FiLM层生成缩放和偏移参数，注入到冻结的Whisper编码器的每个Transformer层中，从而调节中间表征以适应个体病理说话人。同时与全参数微调、参数高效微调（如LoRA、Adapter等）基线进行基准对比，并辅以后处理策略。此外，还评估了自适应后模型在语音相关问题回答任务上的表现。

Result: 基于FiLM的说话人条件化ASR在西班牙语和英语病理语音数据集上，与标准微调和参数高效微调等基线方法相比表现具有竞争力，同时能够保持对非条件化（标准）语音的识别性能，验证了该方法的有效性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 说话人条件化（Speaker Conditioning）是一种有效的病理语音ASR自适应策略，通过FiLM注入说话人信息，可以在不修改基础模型权重的前提下实现与现有自适应方法相当的性能，且不会损害模型在标准语音上的原有能力，为低资源病理语音识别提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) has advanced remarkably for standard speech; however, pathological speech from neurological conditions remains a significant challenge. We investigate speaker conditioning via Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM), injecting x-vector-derived information into each transformer layer of a frozen ASR encoder to adapt internal representations to individual pathological speakers without modifying base model weights. We benchmark this for the ASR task against standard and parameter-efficient fine-tuning baselines, complemented by post-processing, on Spanish and English pathological speech. Additionally, we evaluate if the adapted model preserves the ability to answer speech-related questions. Results show that speaker-conditioned ASR is competitive with established adaptation strategies while retaining performance on non-conditioned speech.

</details>


### [187] [Benchmarking Open-Source Layout Detection Models for Data Snapshot Extraction from Institutional Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06242)
*AJ Carl P. Dy,Aivin V. Solatorio*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向机构文档中"数据快照提取"任务的基准数据集与评估框架，标注了人道主义报告、世行政策研究论文和项目评估文档中包含可复用分析信息的图表，并评估了多个开源布局检测模型在运营性机构文档上的表现，揭示了通用文档布局分析与实际可用的数据快照提取之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前从文档中提取视觉内容的方法主要基于通用文档布局分析，将图表视为统一的文档对象，而非具有语义意义的分析性人工制品。机构文档中存在大量嵌入在图表中的可复用运营和分析信息，但缺乏专门针对"语义上有意义的视觉人工制品"识别与定位的基准和评估框架。

Method: 构建了一个包含人道主义报告、世界银行政策研究工作论文和项目评估文档的基准数据集，对其中包含可复用分析信息的图表进行了标注；定义了"数据快照提取"任务；并利用该数据集对多个开源布局检测模型进行了基准测试，从检测性能和空间提取质量两个维度进行评估。

Result: 当前模型在传统学术基准上表现强劲，但在运营性机构文档上泛化能力不足。常见失败模式包括：分析性内容与非分析性内容的混淆、复合分析性人工制品的碎片化、以及解读所需的上下文信息提取不完整。

Conclusion: 通用文档布局分析与运营性可用的数据快照提取之间存在持续差距，需要专门针对机构文档中语义有意义的分析性图表研发更精准的提取方法。作者公开了PDF源文件、标注数据集、元数据和源代码以支持未来研究。

Abstract: Institutional documents contain substantial amounts of operational and analytical information embedded within figures and tables. Current approaches for extracting visual content from documents are largely built around generic document layout analysis, where figures and tables are treated as uniformly relevant document objects rather than semantically meaningful analytical artifacts. In this work, we introduce a benchmark dataset and evaluation framework for \textit{data snapshot extraction}, the task of identifying and localizing semantically meaningful visual artifacts within institutional documents. The benchmark spans humanitarian reports, World Bank policy research working papers, and project appraisal documents, and includes annotations for figures and tables that contain reusable analytical information. Using this dataset, we benchmarked multiple open-source layout detection models and evaluated both detection performance and spatial extraction quality. Our results show that current models struggle to generalize to operational institutional documents despite strong performance on conventional academic benchmarks. Common failure modes include confusion between analytical and non-analytical content, fragmentation of composite analytical artifacts, and incomplete extraction of contextual information required for interpretation. These findings highlight a persistent gap between generic document layout analysis and operationally useful data snapshot extraction. We release the source PDFs, annotation dataset, metadata, and source code to support future research in operational document intelligence. The dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/ai4data/data-snapshot and the source code is available at https://github.com/worldbank/ai4data/tree/main/experimental/data-snapshot.

</details>


### [188] [From Self to Other: Evaluating Demographic Perspective-Taking in LLM Hate Speech Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06266)
*Paloma Piot,Javier Parapar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了人格条件化的大语言模型在模拟不同人口群体对仇恨言论判断差异方面的表现，从群体间分歧、内群体敏感性和替代预测三个维度进行考察。结果发现没有任何模型能同时胜任所有维度，但Llama 3.1的替代提示（vicarious prompting）在多数人口轴上给出了最接近人类分歧模式的近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仇恨言论检测具有高度主观性，不同人口群体对同一内容的感知差异很大，但收集足够多的多群体标注成本高昂且难以扩展。已有研究提议用人格条件化大语言模型来大规模模拟多元视角，但其是否真实反映不同群体间的分歧模式尚未被系统验证，这正是本文的研究动机。

Method: 作者定义了人类社会判断的三个维度：（1）群体间分歧——不同群体的人格是否以类人的方式产生分歧；（2）内群体敏感性——当内容针对自身身份时，人格是否变得更加敏感；（3）替代预测——人格能否准确预测另一群体的反应。在此框架下，作者对多种大语言模型和提示策略进行了系统评估，包括最小身份提示和替代提示（让模型模拟另一群体的视角）。

Result: 主要发现包括：没有任何模型能一致地同时捕捉三个维度；模型表现高度依赖于具体模型选择，且仅靠最小身份提示无法可靠地产生类人的分歧模式；但Llama 3.1在使用替代提示时，在多数人口统计轴上获得了最高的跨群体一致性，提供了与人类分歧模式最接近的近似，表明该配置可能是对齐人类判断的更可靠自动标注方案。

Conclusion: 人格条件化LLM在模拟人类对仇恨言论的社会判断分歧方面存在显著局限，不能简单地依赖最小身份提示。替代提示（vicarious prompting）配合Llama 3.1等特定模型，提供了一条更有前景的路径，有望实现与人类判断对齐的可扩展自动标注。

Abstract: Hate speech detection is inherently subjective: people from different demographic groups perceive the same content very differently. Collecting enough annotations from multiple demographic groups is costly and difficult to scale. Persona-conditioned Large Language Models (models prompted to adopt a specific demographic identity) have been proposed as a way to simulate diverse perspectives at scale. But do they actually reflect how different groups disagree? We evaluate three aspects of human social judgement: (i) whether personas from different groups disagree in human-like ways (inter-group disagreement), (ii) whether they become more sensitive when content targets their own identity (in-group sensitivity), and (iii) whether they can accurately predict how another group would react (vicarious prediction). Our results show that no model consistently captures all three dimensions, and performance is highly model-dependent and does not emerge reliably from minimal identity prompts alone. However, vicarious prompting with Llama 3.1 yields the highest cross-group agreement in most demographic axes and provides the closest overall approximation to human disagreement patterns, indicating that this configuration may provide a more reliable setting for automatic annotation aligned with human judgements.

</details>


### [189] [Many Circuits, One Mechanism: Input Variation and Evaluation Granularity in Circuit Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06267)
*Alireza Bayat Makou,Jingcheng Niu,Subhabrata Dutta,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了“幻象特化”现象：在固定任务下，仅改变输入统计特征所导出的电路在结构上看似不同，但功能上等价。通过对Literal Sequence Copying任务在不同词频条件下的实验，作者发现结构差异并不对应功能差异，挑战了“结构差异即机制差异”的常见假设，并指出需要边级别评估和跨条件迁移测试来暴露这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电路发现（circuit discovery）方法常被用来识别解释模型行为的子图，研究者普遍将发现的电路之间的结构差异解释为不同机制的证据。本文质疑这一假设，提出结构差异可能仅仅源于输入统计特征的变化，而非真正不同的计算机制，即“幻象特化”。

Method: 作者设计Literal Sequence Copying任务，设置四种词频带（token-frequency bands）加一个控制条件，在五个Pythia模型（70M到1.4B）上共提取75个电路。通过三种方式验证功能等价性：(1) 跨频带迁移边（band-specific边缘是否可泛化）；(2) 共享核心子图能否恢复至少99%的电路性能；(3) 因果互换干预（causal interchange interventions）检验内部表征是否跨频带可互换。此外，对同一频带重复提取电路以检验发现算法的唯一性。

Result: 结果发现：(1) 结构不同的电路实现了相同的计算；(2) 一个跨大多数频带共享的核心子图可恢复至少99%的电路性能；(3) 因果互换干预确认内部表征跨频带可互换；(4) 同一频带内重复提取表明发现算法从等价有效子图类中采样，而非恢复唯一机制；(5) 源级别（source-level）评估会虚高忠实度，而边级别（edge-level）评估能揭示结构到功能的多对一映射。

Conclusion: 电路间的结构差异不足以作为不同机制的证据。要暴露幻象特化，必须采用边级别评估和跨条件迁移测试，而非仅依赖源级别评估。电路发现算法本质上是从一个等价类中进行采样，而非恢复唯一解。

Abstract: Circuit discovery methods identify subgraphs that explain specific model behaviors, and structural differences between discovered circuits are commonly interpreted as evidence of distinct mechanisms. We test this assumption by varying input statistics while holding the task fixed, and show that the resulting structural differences exhibit apparent specialization but do not correspond to functional differences, a pattern we term phantom specialization. Using Literal Sequence Copying across four token-frequency bands plus a control condition in five Pythia models (70M-1.4B), we extract 75 circuits and find that structurally distinct circuits implement the same computation: band-specific edges transfer broadly across bands, a core shared across most bands recovers at least 99% of circuit performance, and causal interchange interventions confirm that internal representations are interchangeable across frequency bands. Repeated extractions within the same frequency band further suggest that discovery algorithms sample from an equivalence class of valid subgraphs rather than recovering a unique mechanism. Standard evaluation practice obscures this pattern: source-level evaluation inflates apparent faithfulness, while edge-level evaluation reveals the many-to-one mapping from structure to function. Our results show that structural differences between circuits are not sufficient evidence for distinct mechanisms, and that exposing this requires edge-level evaluation and cross-condition transfer tests.

</details>


### [190] [FOXGLOVE: Understanding Goal-Oriented and Anchored Writing Feedback from Experts and LLMs on Argumentative Essays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06271)
*Yijun Liu,Yifan Song,John Gallagher,Sarah Sterman,Tal August*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FOXGLOVE是一个包含2340条写作反馈的数据集，系统比较了人类写作教师与四种前沿LLM在目标导向、句子锚定和优先级等维度上的反馈差异，发现两者在反馈目标和文章位置的分布上相似，但在具体句子选择上存在分歧；LLM反馈更长、更复杂、评分更高，但优势主要归因于篇幅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM被广泛用于生成写作反馈，但目前缺乏在写作研究核心维度（目标导向、具体句子锚定、优先级排序）上对LLM与专家反馈的系统性比较。填补这一空白，为理解人机反馈的对齐与分歧提供实证基础。

Method: 构建FOXGLOVE数据集：收集69篇12年级议论文的696条写作教师反馈，并在统一协议下由四款前沿LLM生成1644条反馈，共2340条评论；由专家对教师和LLM的反馈子集进行质量评分；从目标导向、句子锚定、优先级等维度进行系统对比分析。

Result: 教师与LLM在反馈目标和文章位置的分布上相似，但在具体锚定句子的选择上存在显著分歧；LLM反馈更复杂、更少使用问句；LLM反馈在多数质量维度上评分更高，但这一优势很大程度上归因于其反馈篇幅更长。

Conclusion: FOXGLOVE数据集揭示了人类与LLM写作反馈在宏观分布上趋同、在微观句子定位上分化的特征，且LLM的表面质量优势可能源于长度效应，为写作反馈系统的设计与评估提供了新的基准和洞察。

Abstract: While large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate writing feedback, there remains no systematic comparison of LLM and expert feedback on the dimensions that writing research identifies as central to revision: goal-orientation, anchoring to specific sentences, and prioritization. We introduce FOXGLOVE, a dataset of 696 feedback comments written by trained writing instructors on 69 twelfth-grade argumentative essays, paired with 1,644 comments generated from four frontier LLMs under a shared protocol, totaling 2,340 comments. We provide expert quality ratings on a subset of both instructor and LLM comments. We find that instructors and LLMs distribute feedback similarly across goals and essay positions, yet instructors and models diverge on the specific sentences on which to provide feedback. Additionally, we find that models tend to write more complex feedback and use fewer questions than instructors. LLM feedback also receives higher ratings on most dimensions of quality, as rated by instructors, but much of this advantage appears to be attributable to lengthier comments. FOXGLOVE enables systematic comparison of where human and LLM feedback align, diverge, and differ.

</details>


### [191] [LLMs Can Leak Training Data But Do They Want To? A Propensity-Aware Evaluation of Memorization in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06286)
*Gianluca Barmina,Peter Schneider-Kamp,Lukas Galke Poech*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PropMe框架与SimpleTrace追踪管线，区分记忆的"能力"与"倾向"，发现大模型在对抗性前缀攻击下能复现训练数据，但在常规使用场景下记忆倾向很低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆评估大多测量模型是否可以被强制复现训练数据，而非测量模型在正常使用下是否会自然泄露。需要区分"提取能力"与"泄露倾向"两个维度，以更全面地理解模型记忆现象。

Method: 提出PropMe倾向感知记忆评估框架，通过度量变换将现有记忆函数转化为倾向度量；设计SimpleTrace轻量级追踪管线，基于infini-gram将模型生成确定性归因到大规模训练语料，计算verbatim、near-verbatim及倾向变换后的记忆指标。在两个全开源模型（Comma和DFM Decoder）及两个数据集（Common Pile和Dynaword）上评估。

Result: 前缀攻击引发的记忆信号显著强于通用或数据集特定提示，但倾向分数整体保持低位。DFM Decoder（从Comma持续预训练而来）在Common Pile上记忆能力和倾向均降低，表明后续训练侧重不同数据可降低先前数据的记忆。

Conclusion: 记忆审计应同时报告最坏情况下的可提取性和常规使用下的泄露倾向，以获得对记忆现象的更全面认识。

Abstract: Large language models can reproduce training data, but existing memorization evaluations mostly measure whether models can be forced to do so, rather than whether they do so under ordinary use. We introduce PropMe, a propensity-aware framework for memorization evaluation that contrasts prefix-based capability attacks with non-adversarial evaluations. We propose a metric transformation that, applied to existing functions, allows to create propensity metrics. We further introduce SimpleTrace, a lightweight tracing pipeline built on infini-gram that deterministically attributes model generations to large-scale training corpora and computes verbatim, near-verbatim, and propensity-transformed memorization metrics. Evaluating two fully-open models: Comma and DFM Decoder on two datasets: Common Pile and Dynaword in two languages, we find a consistent gap between capability and propensity: prefix attacks elicit substantially stronger memorization signals than generic or dataset-specific prompts, while propensity scores remain low overall. Thus, the models can reveal training data when directly elicited, but rarely do so in more common non-adversarial settings. We also find that DFM Decoder, which is continually pre-trained from Comma, exhibits reduced memorization and memorization propensity for Common Pile, confirming that memorization capability can decrease when later training emphasizes partially different data. Our results suggest, and we encourage, that memorization audits should report both worst-case extractability and ordinary leakage propensity in order to have a more comprehensive view of this phenomenon.

</details>


### [192] [Decomposing Factual Sycophancy in Language Models: How Size and Instruction Tuning Shape Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06306)
*Victor De Marez,Luna De Bruyne,Walter Daelemans*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 事实性谄媚（factual sycophancy）可分解为两个机制：模型对真相的基线偏好强度（真相边际，truth margin），以及社会压力对其的偏移程度（操控敏感性，manipulation sensitivity）。作者在56个开源模型（0.3B–32B参数，13种操控类型）上分析发现，脆弱性主要由模型规模决定，但指令微调会改变规模的作用方式。指令微调主要通过提高真相边际来增强鲁棒性，但其行为效应取决于操控类型。基座模型随规模增大获得更高的真相边际但操控敏感性略有上升，而指令微调模型则边际增长更快且敏感性下降。因此，事实性谄媚不是单一标量属性，评估应报告分通道、分操控类型、分规模的鲁棒性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究用"翻转率"（flip rate）来衡量事实性谄媚，但翻转率混淆了两个独立机制：模型本身的真相偏好强度，以及模型对社会压力的敏感程度。单一的翻转率指标无法区分这两种机制，导致无法准确诊断模型的脆弱性来源，也无法精细比较不同规模和训练方式的模型。

Method: 将事实性谄媚分解为"真相边际"和"操控敏感性"两个通道。在56个开源模型（参数规模0.3B到32B）上，使用13种操控类型进行系统实验。分别考察基座模型和指令微调模型，分析规模扩展和指令微调对这两个通道的差异化影响。

Result: 1）脆弱性主要由模型规模决定，但指令微调改变了规模的影响模式；2）小模型指令微调后可能更不鲁棒，大模型指令微调后通常更鲁棒；3）指令微调主要提升真相边际，但行为效应依赖操控类型；4）规模扩展对两个通道的影响不同：基座模型获得更高边际但操控敏感性略增，指令微调模型边际增长更快且敏感性下降。

Conclusion: 事实性谄媚不是单一标量属性，不应仅用翻转率衡量。评估应报告分通道（真相边际和操控敏感性）、分操控类型、分规模条件下的鲁棒性，以更精细地诊断和改进模型行为。

Abstract: Factual sycophancy occurs when a language model abandons a correct, verifiable answer under social pressure. Because a flip occurs only when pressure toward a false answer exceeds the model's neutral preference for the truth, flip rates conflate two mechanisms: the strength of that baseline preference (truth margin), and how far pressure shifts it (manipulation sensitivity). We decompose factual sycophancy into these channels and use them to separate the effects of size and instruction tuning across 56 open-weight models spanning 0.3B-32B parameters and 13 manipulation types. We find that vulnerability is governed mainly by size, but instruction tuning changes how size acts: small instruction-tuned models can become less robust, whereas large instruction-tuned models usually become more robust. Instruction tuning primarily increases truth margin, but its behavioral effect depends on manipulation type. Scaling also changes the two channels differently: base models gain margin but become mildly more manipulation-sensitive, whereas instruction-tuned models gain margin faster and become less sensitive. Factual sycophancy is therefore not a single scalar property. Evaluations should report channel-specific, manipulation-specific, and size-conditioned robustness rather than flip rates alone.

</details>


### [193] ["Chi nas dal soch el sent de legn" -- Auditing Text Corpora for Lombard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06349)
*Edoardo Signoroni,Pavel Rychlý*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对低资源语言伦巴第语的平行语料库和单语语料库进行了人工审计，发现大规模网络爬取数据中存在严重的语言误识别、模板文本和非语言噪声问题，且高质量数据严重偏向西部变体，东部变体被边缘化，呼吁采用变体感知、社区驱动的数据策展方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界上许多语言在NLP工具方面仍然资源不足，主要原因是缺乏高质量数据集。伦巴第语作为一个低资源语言连续体，表面上有丰富的网络爬取数据，但实际质量存疑。作者旨在揭示这些数据的真实质量状况，并分析其中的系统性偏差。

Method: 作者对伦巴第语可用的平行语料库和单语语料库进行了系统性人工审计。具体包括：(1) 对网络爬取数据集、策展语料库和基准数据集进行语言识别质量评估；(2) 分析有效伦巴第语部分的拼写正字法组成；(3) 考察不同变体（西部与东部伦巴第语）在不同类型语料库中的分布和代表性。

Result: (1) 大规模网络爬取数据集中充斥着语言误识别、模板文本和非语言噪声，表面上丰富的数据实为假象；(2) 各数据集之间存在相互矛盾的正字法系统；(3) 高质量数据严重偏向西部伦巴第变体，东部变体在各类语料库中均处于边缘地位，存在显著的代表性偏差。

Conclusion: 对于低资源语言的数据策展，单纯追求数量驱动的网络爬取策略存在严重缺陷。应当采用变体感知（variety-aware）、社区驱动（community-driven）的数据策展方式，以确保数据的质量和语言变体的公平代表性。

Abstract: Several of the world's languages are still under-resourced in terms of Natural Language Processing (NLP) tools. This is mostly due to the lack of high-quality datasets to train, develop, and evaluate systems and models for several tasks, such as Machine Translation (MT). We conduct a manual audit of the parallel and monolingual corpora available for Lombard, an under-resourced language continuum from Italy. Our analysis reveals that the perceived abundance of web-scraped data is an illusion, with massive datasets plagued by severe language misidentification, boilerplate text, and non-linguistic noise. Furthermore, we analyze the orthographic composition of the valid Lombard portions across web-scraped datasets, curated corpora, and benchmarks. Our findings show conflicting orthographical systems and severe representational bias across all corpora: high-quality data is heavily skewed towards Western Lombard varieties, with Eastern ones left on the margins. This underscores the need for variety-aware, community-driven data curation rather than purely quantity-driven scraping.

</details>


### [194] [EDIT: Evidence-Diagnosed Intervention Training for Rule-Faithful LLM Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06350)
*Zhihao Wu,Linhai Zhang,Taiyi Wang,Runcong Zhao,Peter Andrews,Cesare Aloisi,Yulan He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EDIT 是一个两阶段框架，通过定位并修正评分推理中的错误步骤，并利用信念引导的奖励塑形进行强化学习，训练出更忠实于评分量表的 LLM 评分器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评分方法（如信用分配和干预方法）主要针对数学推理等自包含任务设计，无法定位评分推理中哪里出错，也无法追踪模型对最终分数的信念变化。因此需要一种能诊断并修正评分推理错误的新方法。

Method: 提出 EDIT 两阶段框架：(1) EDIT-SFT：利用模型内部信号（对最终分数的后验信念和输入-依据对齐分数）定位有问题的推理步骤，借助评分量表清单仅修正这些局部步骤；(2) EDIT-RL：使用信念引导的奖励塑形进行校准，惩罚有害的大幅信念漂移，同时保留有益的探索。

Result: 在两个真实世界多学科评分基准上，EDIT 在域内和域外划分上均一致优于强监督微调和强化学习基线，消融研究证实内部状态诊断是性能提升的关键驱动因素。

Conclusion: 通过结合内部状态诊断与信念引导训练，EDIT 能有效提升 LLM 评分器的量表忠实度，为自动化评分提供更可靠、可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Reliable rubric grading requires more than accurate score prediction. Each judgement must be grounded in the mark scheme and evidence from the student answer. Existing credit-assignment and intervention methods, primarily designed for self-contained reasoning tasks such as mathematics reasoning, struggle in this setting because they do not identify where grading reasoning goes wrong or how the model's belief about the final mark changes during reasoning. We propose Evidence-Diagnosed Intervention Training (EDIT), a two-phase framework for training more rubric-faithful LLM graders. First, EDIT-SFT locates problematic reasoning steps using internal model signals: posterior belief over the final mark and input-grounding scores. It then revises only these local steps with help from a rubric checklist. Second, EDIT-RL calibrates the grader with belief-guided reward shaping, penalising large harmful belief drifts while still allowing helpful exploration. Experiments on two real-world, multi-subject grading benchmarks demonstrate that EDIT consistently outperforms strong supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning baselines on both in-domain and out-of-domain splits, with ablation studies confirming that internal-state diagnostics drive these gains.

</details>


### [195] [Emergent Language as an Approach to Conscious AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06380)
*Zengqing Wu,Chuan Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种生成式方法论，利用多智能体强化学习中的涌现语言来研究人工系统意识。通过在最小环境中剥离人类语言先验，作者发现智能体自发产生了自我指涉通信和回声失配检测回路，证明了该方法可用于研究意识相关结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究人工意识的两类方法——判别式（基于理论清单评估）和架构式（直接工程化意识模块）——都存在一个根本缺陷：观察到的结构可能是人类语言先验的产物，而非系统自身的涌现属性。因此需要一种能排除人类语言先验污染、使因果可溯源至任务需求本身的方法。

Method: 提出生成式方法论：在多智能体强化学习中设置涌现语言（EL）场景。智能体从最小起点出发——无语言、无自我概念、极少接触人类文本——仅在任务压力下通过交互发展通信。通过控制环境复杂度和可供给性，研究者可以观察意识相关结构是否自发涌现，并将因果归因于任务需求而非人类语言先验。

Result: 在最小环境的概念验证中，智能体发展出了自我指涉通信（self-referential communication），包括一个回声失配检测回路（echo-mismatch detection circuit）。该回路并非由任务结构或架构单独预测，而是从特定的环境可供给性中涌现。

Conclusion: 涌现语言作为一种生成式工具，能够有效剥离人类语言先验，为研究意识相关结构提供因果可溯源的方法论路径。自我指涉通信的涌现表明，即便在最小环境中，任务压力也可催生与意识相关的结构。

Abstract: The question of whether artificial systems can be conscious remains open, in part because existing approaches either evaluate systems against theory-derived checklists (discriminative) or engineer consciousness-inspired modules directly (architectural); both leave open whether observed structures are artifacts of human language priors. We propose a generative methodology: emergent language (EL) in multi-agent reinforcement learning, where agents start from minimal (no language, no concept of self, minimal exposure to human text) and develop communication under task pressure alone, ensuring causal attributability to task demands rather than inherited human language priors. We position our methodology by discussing how EL serves as a generative tool for studying consciousness-relevant structure, including the role of environment complexity and the interpretation of emergent communication. As a proof of concept, we instantiate this methodology in a minimal environment and show that agents develop self-referential communication, including an echo-mismatch detection circuit that is not predicted by task structure or architecture alone but emerges from a specific environmental affordance.

</details>


### [196] [CollabSim: A CSCW-Grounded Methodology for Investigating Collaborative Competence of LLM Agents through Controlled Multi-Agent Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06399)
*Jiaju Chen,Bo Sun,Yuxuan Lu,Yun Wang,Dakuo Wang,Bingsheng Yao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了CollabSim，一个基于CSCW理论的模拟框架，用于系统评估多智能体系统中LLM智能体的协作能力，而非单纯的个体任务能力。实验表明该框架能捕捉交互条件效应、区分模型表现模式并揭示任务依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体系统评估主要关注任务结果或单智能体能力（推理、规划、工具使用），却忽略了智能体在协作中的关键能力：建立共同基础、维持共享任务理解、平衡个体与集体激励、修复对齐偏差。这些恰恰是CSCW研究数十年所强调的人类团队协作核心要求。

Method: 提出CollabSim框架，包含三个核心设计：(1)基于CSCW理论的协作能力定义，将协作能力形式化为可测量的维度；(2)对交互条件（如通信约束）进行受控操纵；(3)在动作粒度上探测智能体的内部状态。在四款LLM上进行实验验证。

Result: CollabSim能够有效捕获不同交互条件下的效应，分离出各模型的协作表现模式，并揭示智能体设计带来的任务依赖性效应。验证了框架对MAS协作能力系统分析的有效性。

Conclusion: 评估MAS不能仅看任务完成度或个体能力，协作能力（common ground、shared understanding、incentive alignment、misalignment repair）同样关键且可被系统衡量。CollabSim为MAS协作能力的诊断与改进提供了理论驱动的评估基础设施。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) built on large language models have shown growing promise, with their effectiveness resting on agents' ability to coordinate through text-based channels much as human teams do. Yet recent study suggests that MAS often falter not because agents lack individual task-solving ability, but because they lack collaborative competence: the capacity to establish common ground, maintain shared task understanding, balance individual and collective incentives, and repair misalignment as interaction unfolds. Decades of research in Computer-Supported Cooperative Work have characterized these requirements for human teams coordinating under constrained communication, yet existing MAS evaluations focus mainly on task outcomes or single-agent proficiency in reasoning, planning, and tool use. To enable a systematic analysis of agents' collaborative competence in MAS, we introduce CollabSim, a configurable simulation framework that combines a theory-grounded definition of collaborative capabilities, controlled manipulation of interaction conditions, and action-level probing of agents' internal states. Experiments across four LLMs show that CollabSim can capture condition effects, separate model performance patterns, and reveal task-dependent effects of agent design.

</details>


### [197] [A Komi-Yazva--Russian Parallel Corpus and Evaluation Protocol for Zero- and Few-Shot LLM Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06420)
*Petr Parshakov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 首次构建科米-亚兹瓦语（Komi-Yazva）与俄语的平行语料库，并提出一套面向濒危语言、极低资源场景下大语言模型翻译的评估协议，系统比较了零样本与检索增强少样本两种提示策略下的模型表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 濒危语言平行数据极度稀缺，缺乏标准化的翻译评估基准。科米-亚兹瓦语作为极度濒危的乌拉尔语系语言，此前无公开平行语料，无法系统评估大语言模型在此类极低资源条件下的翻译能力与评估方法。

Method: 构建包含457个对齐句对（来自74篇叙事文本）的平行语料库，附带出处、故事ID和句子级对齐。设计故事级交叉验证、确定性检索增强少样本提示、严格输出验证、基于参考译文和基于裁判模型的互补指标，以及故事级不确定性估计，实现防泄漏评估。

Result: 大语言模型可产生非平凡译文，但性能因模型家族和提示策略差异显著；检索增强少样本提示一致优于零样本提示，但超出小规模检索上下文后增益有限；评估结论显著依赖于指标选择与失败处理方式。

Conclusion: 该语料库既是数据贡献，也可作为濒危语言机器翻译的可复现评估试验台；在极低资源场景下，评估方法论（指标选择、失败处理、故事级交叉验证）对结论具有实质性影响。

Abstract: We present the first Komi-Yazva--Russian parallel corpus together with an explicit evaluation protocol for studying LLM translation in an endangered, extremely low-resource setting. The dataset contains 457 aligned sentence pairs from 74 narrative texts and is accompanied by documented provenance, sentence-level alignment, and story identifiers that enable leakage-aware evaluation. We use this setup to compare modern large language models on Komi-Yazva-to-Russian translation under severe parallel-data scarcity in zero-shot and retrieval-based few-shot regimes. The protocol includes story-level cross-validation, deterministic retrieval for few-shot prompting, strict validation of generated outputs, complementary reference-based and judge-based metrics, and story-level uncertainty estimates. Across models, LLMs produce non-trivial translations, but performance varies strongly by model family and prompting regime. Retrieval-based few-shot prompting consistently improves over zero-shot prompting, while gains beyond a small retrieved context remain limited. The results show that evaluative conclusions in this setting depend materially on metric choice and failure handling, so the paper frames the corpus as both a dataset contribution and a reproducible evaluation testbed for endangered-language machine translation.

</details>


### [198] [Reinforcement Learning Elicits Contextual Learning of Unseen Language Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06428)
*Hanxu Hu,Zdeněk Šnajdr,Pinzhen Chen,Jannis Vamvas,Rico Sennrich*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种强化学习方法，以chrF为奖励信号训练LLM利用上下文中的语言学知识翻译极低资源语言，在完全未见过的语言上表现优于上下文学习和监督微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（继续训练或在上下文中编码语法书）在翻译低资源语言时容易过拟合特定语言，零样本迁移能力有限。作者认为LLM需要掌握利用上下文语言学知识的元技能，而非记忆特定语言，以实现大规模极低资源语言翻译。

Method: 提出基于强化学习的未见语言翻译方法：为模型提供丰富的语言学上下文（如语法书描述），以表层翻译指标chrF作为奖励信号，通过强化学习训练模型从上下文中提取并应用相关语言学信息进行翻译。

Result: 尽管仅使用轻量级的chrF奖励，RL训练的模型能有效从上下文提取语言学信息，在完全未见过的语言上翻译质量优于上下文学习和监督微调方法。

Conclusion: 基于结果的强化学习不仅适用于数学、编程等传统推理任务，也可作为从上下文进行语言学习的有效方法，为极低资源语言翻译提供了新思路。

Abstract: Prior work has shown that large language models (LLMs) can translate unseen or low-resource languages by undergoing continued training or even by encoding a grammar book in their context. However, both methods typically overfit specific languages, with limited zero-shot transfer at test time. To translate extremely low-resource languages at scale, we argue that LLMs must acquire the meta-skill of utilizing in-context linguistic knowledge rather than memorizing specific languages. In this paper, we propose a reinforcement learning (RL) approach to unseen language translation given rich linguistic context, using a surface-level translation metric (chrF) as the reward. Empirically, despite the lightweight reward, our RL-trained models effectively extract and apply relevant linguistic information from the provided context, leading to better translations on completely unseen languages than in-context learning or supervised fine-tuning. Our analyses suggest that outcome-based RL can extend beyond conventional reasoning tasks like math and coding to serve as a recipe for language learning from context.

</details>


### [199] [Revising Context, Shifting Simulated Stance: Auditing LLM-Based Stance Simulation in Online Discussions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06443)
*Xinnong Zhang,Wanting Shan,Hanjia Lyu,Zhongyu Wei,Jiebo Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于反事实上下文修订的审计框架，用于评估大语言模型在立场模拟中对对话上下文的敏感度，发现文本和多模态修订策略均能有效且稳健地改变模拟用户立场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地被用于模拟社交媒体用户并推断其对在线讨论的反应，但目前尚不清楚这些模拟是反映了精确的用户特定信念，还是高度敏感于语义无关的对话上下文变化。因此需要一种系统性的审计方法来评估LLM立场模拟的上下文敏感性。

Method: 作者提出反事实上下文修订框架：首先从原始在线对话中推断目标用户对特定话题的立场，然后对对话上下文施加受控的修订策略（包括纯文本修订和融入meme的多模态修订），再次模拟用户立场，最后通过平均方向性立场偏移量和立场转换率两个指标评估修订效果，并对比不同极化偏好机制下的表现。

Result: 实验结果表明，无论是纯文本策略还是多模态策略，在不同极化偏好机制下均能实现有效且稳健的立场转换，揭示了LLM立场模拟对上下文修订的高度敏感性。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解LLM立场模拟的上下文敏感性提供了评估框架，同时揭示了使用LLM模拟在线舆论动态的前景与风险。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to simulate social media users and infer how individuals may respond to online discussions. However, it remains unclear whether these simulations reflect precise user-specific beliefs or whether they are highly sensitive to semantically independent changes in conversational contexts. In this work, we study counterfactual context revision as a framework for auditing LLM-based stance simulation. Given an original online conversation, we first infer a target user's stance toward a specific topic. We then apply controlled revision strategies to the conversational context and simulate the user's stance again under the revised context. We compare text-only revision strategies with a multimodal one that incorporates meme-based context and evaluate two main effectiveness metrics, i.e., average directional stance shift and stance transition rate. The results reveal effective and robust stance transitions in both text-only and multimodal strategies across different polarization-preference mechanisms. Our study contributes an evaluation framework for understanding the context sensitivity of LLM-based stance simulation. More broadly, it highlights both the promise and risk of using LLMs to simulate online opinion dynamics.

</details>


### [200] [Latent Reasoning with Normalizing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06447)
*Guancheng Tu,Xiangjun Fu,Suhao Yu,Yao Tang,Haoqiang Kang,Lianhui Qin,Yizhe Zhang,Jiatao Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NF-CoT 提出了一种基于归一化流的潜在推理框架，在保持自回归语言模型原生优势（左到右生成、概率采样、KV缓存兼容、似然可估计）的同时，用紧凑连续状态进行中间推理，在代码生成任务上提升了通过率并降低了推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本形式的思维链（CoT）虽然有效，但存在根本局限：每个推理步骤必须先被逐词生成才能继续，即使底层更新是语义性的、不确定的或仅部分形成的。现有潜在推理方法虽然通过连续状态提供了更高带宽的替代方案，却往往牺牲了CoT在自回归语言模型中的关键优势，如原生左到右生成、概率采样、KV缓存兼容性以及可处理的似然估计。

Method: NF-CoT 框架在LLM骨干网内部实例化一个 TARFlow 风格的归一化流（normalizing flow），对从显式CoT中蒸馏得到的紧凑连续思维（continuous thoughts）建模一个可处理的概率模型。连续思维位置由NF头生成，文本位置由标准LM头在同一因果流中生成。该设计为潜在思维提供精确似然，支持基于原始KV缓存的左到右概率解码，并支持在潜在推理空间中进行直接的策略梯度优化。

Result: 在代码生成基准测试上，NF-CoT 相比显式CoT和先前的潜在推理基线方法提升了通过率（pass rates），同时显著降低了中间推理成本。

Conclusion: NF-CoT 成功地在不牺牲自回归语言模型核心优势的前提下实现了潜在推理，证明了归一化流是建模连续思维的有效工具，为高效、可扩展的潜在推理开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Large language models often improve reasoning by generating explicit chain-of-thought (CoT), demonstrating the importance of intermediate computation. However, textual CoT forces this computation through a discrete, serial, and communication-oriented token stream: each reasoning step must be verbalized before the model can proceed, even when the underlying update is semantic, uncertain, or only partially formed. Latent reasoning offers a higher-bandwidth alternative by performing intermediate computation in compact continuous states before committing to text. Yet existing latent-reasoning methods often sacrifice key advantages that make CoT effective in autoregressive language models, including native left-to-right generation, probabilistic sampling, compatibility with KV-cache decoding, and tractable likelihood estimation. We propose NF-CoT, a latent reasoning framework that preserves these advantages by modeling continuous thoughts with normalizing flows. NF-CoT instantiates a TARFlow-style normalizing flow inside the LLM backbone, defining a tractable probability model over compact continuous thoughts distilled from explicit CoT. Continuous-thought positions are generated by an NF head, while text positions are generated by the standard LM head within the same causal stream. This design provides exact likelihoods for latent thoughts, enables probabilistic left-to-right decoding with the original KV cache, and supports direct policy-gradient optimization in the latent reasoning space. On code-generation benchmarks, NF-CoT improves pass rates over explicit-CoT and prior latent-reasoning baselines while substantially reducing intermediate-reasoning cost.

</details>


### [201] [Human Adults and LLMs as Scientists: Who Benefits from Active Exploration?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06464)
*Mandana Samiei,Eunice Yiu,Anthony GX-Chen,Dongyan Lin,Jocelyn Shen,Blake A. Richards,Alison Gopnik,Doina Precup*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 成人因果学习中长期存在"合取劣势"现象，即识别合取因果规则（需多因同时存在）比析取规则更困难。本文探究主动探索是否能改善这一偏见，发现主动干预显著提升合取推理能力，但合取仍需要更多测试；同时将人类表现与大型语言模型对比，发现顶尖模型在推理准确度上接近人类，但探索效率较低且存在类似的合取-析取差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果学习文献中长期观察到的"合取劣势"（conjunctive handicap）大多基于被动观察范式，学习者对证据生成无控制权。本文旨在检验当学习者通过主动探索获得能动性（agency）时，这一偏见是否持续存在，从而填补主动学习情境下合取因果推理研究的空白。

Method: 采用改良版"blicket detector"任务范式。成人参与者可自由干预（主动探索）以识别合取或析取规则结构下的因果对象。同时，将人类表现与多种大型语言模型（LLMs）在同一任务设置下进行系统比较，考察假设推理准确度和探索策略效率。

Result: 主动探索显著提升成人的合取因果推理能力；但合取规则相比析取规则仍需更多测试才能推断。部分最先进的大语言模型在假设推理准确度上接近人类水平，但探索策略效率较低，且同样表现出合取-析取之间的表现差距。

Conclusion: 能动性（主动探索）可部分缓解但未完全消除合取因果推理的困难。大语言模型虽能接近人类推理准确度，但探索策略与人类存在差异，提示当前LLM在因果学习中的主动探索能力仍有改进空间。

Abstract: A long-standing finding in the causal learning literature is that adults struggle to identify conjunctive causal rules, where an effect requires the simultaneous presence of multiple causes, while performing better in disjunctive settings. However, most demonstrations of this ``conjunctive handicap'' rely on passive observation paradigms with limited evidence, where learners have no control over evidence generation. This paper asks whether this bias persists when adults are granted agency through active exploration. Using a modified ``blicket detector'' task, adult participants freely intervened to identify causal objects under conjunctive or disjunctive rule structures. We show that active exploration substantially improves adults' conjunctive causal reasoning, although conjunctive rules still require more tests to infer than disjunctive rules. We further compare human performance to a range of large language models in the same setting. While some state-of-the-art models approach human-level performance on hypothesis inference accuracy, they often exhibit less efficient exploration strategies and similar conjunctive-disjunctive performance gaps.

</details>


### [202] [You Only Index Once: Cross-Layer Sparse Attention with Shared Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06467)
*Yutao Sun,Yanqi Zhang,Li Dong,Jianyong Wang,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CLSA提出跨层稀疏注意力机制，在KV共享架构上复用路由索引，以极低开销实现token级稀疏注意力，在128K上下文下解码加速7.6倍、整体吞吐提升17.1倍，兼顾质量与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM推理面临解码效率瓶颈，现有稀疏注意力存在效率-质量权衡困境：结构化块稀疏加速强但质量损失明显，token级稀疏更准确但因需在全量缓存上做top-k路由而端到端加速有限。

Method: 基于YOCO等KV共享架构，提出跨层稀疏注意力（CLSA）。核心创新是将KV缓存和路由索引同时跨解码层共享：单一索引器计算一次token级top-k选择，生成的稀疏索引复用于所有层，从而保留细粒度token选择性，同时摊薄路由开销。该方法联合优化预填充、KV缓存存储和长上下文解码三个推理瓶颈。

Result: 在短上下文和长上下文基准测试中，CLSA在保持模型质量的同时实现显著效率提升：128K上下文下解码加速高达7.6倍，整体吞吐量提升达17.1倍。

Conclusion: CLSA为长上下文LLM提供了一个更完整的架构解决方案，能够同时提升模型质量与推理效率，突破了稀疏注意力中长期存在的效率-质量权衡困境。

Abstract: Long-context inference in modern LLMs is increasingly constrained by decoding efficiency, especially in reasoning-heavy settings where models generate long intermediate chains of thought. Existing sparse attention methods often face a practical efficiency-quality trade-off. Structured block sparse methods typically provide stronger acceleration but incur noticeable quality loss, while token sparse methods are usually more accurate yet deliver limited end-to-end speedup because top-k routing over the full cache remains expensive. In this work, we propose cross-layer sparse attention (CLSA), which is built on top of KV-sharing architectures such as YOCO. The core idea is to share not only the KV cache across cross-decoder layers, but also the routing index. A single indexer computes token-level top-k selection once and reuses the resulting index across layers, thereby preserving the fine-grained selectivity of token sparse attention while amortizing the routing overhead. The resulting architecture improves all major inference bottlenecks jointly, including pre-filling, KV-cache storage, and long-context decoding. Experiments across short-context and long-context benchmarks show that CLSA is both accurate and efficient, achieving up to 7.6x decoding speedup and 17.1x overall throughput improvement at 128K context. These results suggest a more complete architectural solution for long-context LLMs that jointly advances model quality and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [203] [Self-Augmenting Retrieval for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06474)
*Paul Jünger,Justin Lovelace,Linxi Zhao,Dongyoung Go,Kilian Q. Weinberger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SARDI 利用离散扩散语言模型去噪过程中被丢弃的低置信度token作为前瞻信号，动态引导检索增强生成，在五个多跳QA基准上以最高8倍吞吐量超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统扩散语言模型在去噪时丢弃低置信度的预测token，但这些token实际上包含了有用的实体信息。作者希望利用这些被忽视的"前瞻token"来提前触发检索，从而在输出最终确定前获取更强的证据支撑。

Method: 提出SARDI框架：在离散扩散模型每一步并行去噪时，收集所有预测token（包括低置信度被丢弃的），将其作为前瞻信号送入任意检索器获取相关文档，再将检索到的证据反馈给扩散过程以改善后续去噪步骤。该方法无需训练、与检索器无关、适用于任何具备推理能力的离散扩散语言模型。

Result: 在五个多跳QA基准测试上，SARDI超越了现有的免训练扩散和自回归检索基线方法，吞吐量提升最高达8倍。

Conclusion: 离散扩散模型中看似无用的低置信度token实则是宝贵的前瞻信号，可用于动态检索增强，显著提升推理效率与效果，为扩散语言模型与RAG的结合开辟了新范式。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion language models generate text by iteratively denoising an entire response in parallel. At each step, they predict tentative tokens for every masked position, committing the confident predictions to the output and discarding the unconfident ones. We show that the discarded tokens are in fact a useful lookahead signal for retrieval-augmented generation: even low-confidence tokens often surface salient entities early in the denoising trajectory, enabling retrieval of stronger evidence before the output is finalized. We exploit this through Self-Augmenting Retrieval for Diffusion Language Models (SARDI), a dynamic RAG framework that uses these lookahead tokens to guide retrieval during denoising. SARDI is training-free, retriever-agnostic, and applicable to any reasoning-capable discrete diffusion language model. Across five multi-hop QA benchmarks, SARDI outperforms current training-free diffusion and autoregressive retrieval baselines at up to $8\times$ higher throughput.

</details>


### [204] [Operation-Guided Progressive Human-to-AI Text Transformation Benchmark for Multi-Granularity AI-Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06481)
*Sondos Mahmoud Bsharat,Jiacheng Liu,Xiaohan Zhao,Tianjun Yao,Xinyi Shang,Yi Tang,Jiacheng Cui,Ahmed Elhagry,Salwa K. Al Khatib,Hao Li,Salman Khan,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: OpAI-Bench 是一个面向渐进式人机协同编辑场景的AI文本检测基准，通过构建从人类撰写到AI逐步修改的九个版本序列，揭示AI可检测性受编辑操作类型、领域和累积修订历史共同影响的非单调规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI文本检测基准主要关注最终输出，忽略了现实场景中普遍存在的渐进式人机协同修订过程，无法揭示AI写作信号如何在修订过程中出现、累积或消失。

Method: 构建OpAI-Bench基准，从人类撰写文档出发，在预设AI覆盖率和五种代表性AI编辑操作下生成九个顺序修订版本，覆盖四个领域，保留文档、句子、token和span多粒度作者溯源信息，并集成8种文档级、7种句子级和2种细粒度检测器进行全面评估。

Result: 实验表明AI文本可检测性不仅取决于AI编辑内容比例，还受编辑操作类型、领域和累积修订历史影响；混合署名的中间版本往往比完全人类撰写和重度AI编辑的两个端点更难检测，呈现非单调检测模式。

Conclusion: OpAI-Bench为分析AI辅助写作在现实渐进式编辑场景下是否、何时以及如何变得可检测提供了可控实验平台，揭露了现有基准所忽视的非单调检测现象。

Abstract: As AI writing assistants become increasingly integrated into real-world drafting and revision workflows, many documents are no longer purely human-written or AI-generated, but instead result from progressive human-AI co-editing. However, existing AI-text detection benchmarks largely focus on final outputs and provide limited understanding of how AI authorship signals emerge, accumulate, or disappear throughout the revision process. We introduce OpAI-Bench, an operation-guided benchmark for studying progressive human-to-AI text transformation across document, sentence, token, and span granularities. Starting from human-written documents, OpAI-Bench constructs nine sequentially revised versions for each sample under predefined AI coverage levels and five representative AI edit operations, covering four domains while preserving complete authorship provenance at multiple granularities. The benchmark supports comprehensive evaluation with 8 document-level detectors, 7 sentence-level detectors, and 2 fine-grained token/span-level detectors. Experiments reveal that AI-text detectability is governed not only by the proportion of AI-edited content, but also by edit operation, domain, and cumulative revision history. Interestingly, we notice that mixed-authorship intermediate versions are often harder to detect than both fully human and heavily AI-edited endpoints, exposing non-monotonic detection patterns missed by existing benchmarks. OpAI-Bench provides a controlled testbed for analyzing whether, when, and how AI-assisted writing becomes detectable under realistic progressive editing scenarios. Our code and benchmark are available at https://github.com/VILA-Lab/OpAI-Bench.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [205] [HyFAD: Hybrid Time-Frequency Diffusion with Frequency-Aware Embedding for Time Series Imputation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05239)
*Hongfan Gao,Wangmeng Shen,Bin Yang,Jilin Hu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: HyFAD 是一种用于时间序列插补的混合时频扩散模型，通过耦合的时域-频域扩散框架（时域捕获低频全局趋势，频域细化高频分量），并引入频率感知步长嵌入来提供步进依赖的频谱引导，在多个基准数据集上达到了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的时间序列方法存在三个核心不足：(1) 对频率敏感的降噪能力欠缺；(2) 高频成分重建质量差；(3) 难以同时平衡全局趋势与局部动态的建模。这促使作者设计一种能够协同利用时域和频域信息的新框架。

Method: HyFAD 基于 DDPM 范式，构建了耦合的时域-频域扩散框架。反向去噪过程从时域到频域顺序进行，实现由粗到细的生成：时域扩散过程负责捕获低频全局趋势，频域扩散过程则精炼高频频谱成分。此外，作者提出了频率感知步长嵌入（Frequency-Aware Step Embedding），利用扩散步数与频谱成分之间的关系，为不同扩散步提供对应的频谱引导，从而实现更精确的逐频带重建。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，HyFAD 达到了最先进的（state-of-the-art）性能。

Conclusion: HyFAD 通过混合时频扩散框架有效解决了现有方法在高频重建和全局-局部平衡方面的不足，频率感知步长嵌入进一步提升了频带级别重建的准确性，验证了时频协同建模在时间序列生成任务中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Diffusion models have demonstrated strong performance in time series modeling due to their ability to progressively capture complex data distributions through iterative denoising. However, existing approaches struggle with frequency-sensitive denoising, high-frequency reconstruction and balancing global trends with local dynamics. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{HyFAD}, a \textbf{Hy}brid time-frequency \textbf{D}iffusion model with \textbf{F}requency-\textbf{A}ware embedding for time series imputation. Built upon the DDPM paradigm, HyFAD adopts a coupled time-frequency diffusion framework, in which the reverse denoising proceeds sequentially from the time domain to the frequency domain, enabling coarse-to-fine generation. Specifically, the time-domain diffusion process captures low-frequency global trends, while the frequency-domain diffusion process refines high-frequency spectral components. We further introduce a frequency-aware step embedding that exploits the relationship between diffusion steps and spectral components, providing step-dependent spectral guidance and facilitates more accurate band-wise reconstruction. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that HyFAD achieves state-of-the-art performance. Our source code is available at https://github.com/hongfangao/HyFAD.

</details>


### [206] [Deterministic Envelopes for Tamed SGLD: Decoupling Stochastic-Gradient Noise and Localizing Taming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05242)
*Yiwei Zhou,Ziheng Chen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 随机梯度Langevin算法中使用与分子相同随机梯度的驯化分母会引入平稳偏差；本文提出结构保持框架，采用固定分母与局部确定性包络来消除该偏差，并设计了一种软-硬混合结构以兼顾稳定性与无偏性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机梯度Langevin算法中，为稳定非全局Lipschitz漂移项常采用驯化分母（tamed denominator），但当分母与分子依赖同一随机梯度实现时，驯化步骤会改变随机预言（stochastic oracle）的性质，即使原始随机梯度是无偏的，也可能产生平稳偏差。现有方法对此缺乏系统认识与解决方案。

Method: 提出结构保持（structure-preserving）框架来设计驯化分母。核心思想是：在采样预言噪声之前先固定分母，并利用局部确定性包络（localized deterministic envelopes）避免在典型区域内不必要的驯化。这些核保留了驯化的稳定效果，同时规避了梯度依赖分母引入的偏差。理论分析将平稳误差分解为两部分：预言依赖驯化引起的偏差，以及确定性稳定化引入的剩余误差。在该确定性包络族内，进一步识别出远尾条件（far-tail condition），揭示局部软包络的局限性，并据此提出混合成员：在典型区域使用软包络，在罕见漂移时由硬尾控制保护。

Result: 实验验证了三方面结论：（1）随机分母确实产生预测的平稳扭曲；（2）确定性包络设计有效降低了偏差；（3）混合结构兼具稳定效果。

Conclusion: 梯度依赖的驯化分母会在随机梯度Langevin算法中引入额外的平稳偏差；通过确定性包络框架固定分母可消除该偏差，而软-硬混合设计在保持稳定性的同时实现了偏差最小化。

Abstract: Stochastic-gradient Langevin algorithms often use tamed denominators to stabilize non-globally Lipschitz drifts. This paper shows that when the denominator depends on the same stochastic-gradient realization as the numerator, the taming step changes the stochastic oracle itself and can create a stationary bias even if the original stochastic gradient is unbiased. We propose a structure-preserving framework for designing tamed denominators. It fixes the denominator before the oracle noise is sampled and uses localized deterministic envelopes to avoid unnecessary taming in typical regions. These kernels keep the stabilizing effect of taming while avoiding the bias introduced by a gradient-dependent denominator. Our theory explains how the stationary error splits into the bias caused by oracle-dependent taming and the remaining error introduced by deterministic stabilization. Within this deterministic-envelope family, the analysis identifies a far-tail condition that explains the limitation of local soft envelopes and motivates a hybrid member: soft in the typical region, but protected by hard-tail control on rare excursions. Experiments confirm the predicted stationary distortions of random denominators, the bias reduction of deterministic-envelope designs, and the stabilizing effect of the hybrid construction.

</details>


### [207] [Harnessing Source Heterogeneity for Cluster-Structured Transfer Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05258)
*Xiaohui Yin,Jun Jin,Shane J. Sacco,Robert H. Aseltine,Kun Chen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对目标人群数据有限但存在多个辅助来源的场景，提出Trans-GLMC方法。该方法通过发现源之间的潜在聚类结构，结合全局融合、簇内精炼和目标去偏倚，在广义线性模型中实现结构化迁移学习，在仿真和自杀风险预测研究中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迁移学习中常面临源异质性问题——不同辅助来源的有用性存在差异，且可能呈现聚类结构。现有方法多将源选择简化为二元的"有用/无用"决策，忽略了可迁移性的分组差异。以康涅狄格州27家医院的自杀风险预测为具体驱动场景：单家医院数据稀疏导致模型不稳定，而简单汇总又抹杀了医院间的差异。

Method: 提出Trans-GLMC流程：（1）基于系数构建目标源与候选源之间的距离度量，恢复潜在的源聚类结构；（2）在广义线性模型框架下，依次进行全局融合、簇内精炼和目标去偏倚，使估计量自适应于检测到的聚类结构。理论上建立了非渐近误差上界。

Result: 理论证明：当存在有意义的聚类结构时，误差界优于无聚类方法；否则退化至与无聚类方法同阶。在仿真和CHIME数据（636,758名患者，27家医院）中，Trans-GLMC提升了针对具体医院的预测精度，识别出具有相互可迁移性的医院社区，并恢复了临床一致的自杀风险因素。

Conclusion: Trans-GLMC通过挖掘源之间的聚类结构，在异质性辅助源场景下实现了更精准的迁移学习，相比传统二元选择方法具有明显优势，具有较强的理论与实用价值。

Abstract: Transfer learning is a natural strategy when a target population has limited data but multiple related auxiliary sources are available. A central difficulty is source heterogeneity: auxiliary sources may not be equally useful, and their usefulness may vary in a structured, cluster-like fashion. Existing transfer-learning methods often reduce source selection to a binary informative/non-informative decision, overlooking subgroups of sources with differential transferability. Motivated by a suicide-risk study using data from the Connecticut Hospital Information Management Exchange (CHIME), comprising 636,758 patients across 27 hospitals, we propose Trans-GLMC, a cluster-structured transfer-learning procedure for generalized linear models. The CHIME setting illustrates the core challenge: hospital-specific risk models are unstable because suicide attempts are rare at any single facility, whereas indiscriminate pooling across hospitals can obscure facility-level differences in patient mix and risk profiles. Trans-GLMC first constructs a coefficient-based distance among the target and candidate sources to recover latent source clusters. It then combines global fusion, within-cluster refinement, and target debiasing to produce an estimator that adapts to the detected structure. We establish a non-asymptotic error bound that improves over its unclustered counterpart whenever a meaningful target cluster exists and matches the unclustered rate up to constants otherwise. In simulations and in the CHIME study, Trans-GLMC improves facility-specific prediction, identifies interpretable communities of hospitals with mutual transferability, and recovers clinically coherent suicide-risk factors.

</details>


### [208] [TabSODA: Tabular Diffusion based Imputation with Skip Pattern Detection and Ordinal Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05361)
*Yuyu Chen,Taehyo Kim,Hai Shu,Yang Feng*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: TabSODA是一个基于Elucidated Diffusion Model的表格数据缺失值插补方法，专门解决大规模问卷数据中的两类问题：结构性跳转（structural skips）不应被插补但常被混淆为无应答，以及有序分类变量被错误当作无序名义变量处理。该方法通过期望最大化（EM）框架，在去噪损失和反向采样中传播结构性跳转，并采用累积probit标量潜变量表征有序变量。在PATH和NSDUH两项全国代表性调查上，TabSODA在MCAR、MAR、MNAR缺失机制下均取得显著的插补精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前表格扩散模型用于大规模调查数据缺失插补时存在两个关键缺陷：(1) 问卷设计导致的结构性跳转（如"不适用"）与真正的项目无应答混为一谈，导致本不该被插补的单元格被错误填充；(2) 有序分类响应（如Likert量表）被不当编码为无序名义变量，丢失了类别间的顺序信息。现有方法缺乏对这两类问题的有效处理机制。

Method: 提出TabSODA（Tabular diffusion with Skip pattern detection and Ordinal Awareness），基于Elucidated Diffusion Model (EDM)框架构建EM式扩散插补器。核心设计：(1) 在去噪损失函数和反向时间采样器中嵌入结构性跳转传播机制，确保跳转单元格不参与梯度更新且不被插补；(2) 对有序变量采用累积probit标量潜变量表示，对名义变量保留analog-bit编码；(3) 当有跳转编码簿时直接使用；否则通过TabSODA+SKIP变体，利用基于CART的跳转模式挖掘器从原始响应和问卷顺序中估计跳转掩码。

Result: 在PATH和NSDUH两项美国全国代表性调查数据集上评估：TabSODA相比最强基线方法，在MCAR、MAR、MNAR三种缺失机制下，有序变量的MACE指标降低高达23.7%，分类准确率提升高达9%。跳转模式挖掘器在两个数据集上均达到接近完美的精确率，使得TabSODA+SKIP的性能与使用真实编码簿掩码的版本非常接近。

Conclusion: TabSODA通过同时解决结构性跳转检测和有序变量感知两大挑战，显著提升了大规模调查数据缺失插补的质量。即使在没有先验跳转编码簿的情况下，自动挖掘的跳转掩码也能使模型性能接近理想上界。该方法为问卷调查研究中的缺失数据处理提供了更准确、更符合数据生成机制的解决方案。

Abstract: Missing data imputation in large-scale surveys faces two challenges that are not well handled by current tabular diffusion methods. First, \emph{structural skips}, cells made inapplicable by questionnaire design, should not be imputed but are often conflated with item nonresponse. Second, \emph{ordinal} responses encode ordered categories, yet most pipelines treat them as nominal levels through one-hot or analog-bit encodings. We introduce \textbf{TabSODA} (\textbf{Tab}ular diffusion with \textbf{S}kip pattern detection and \textbf{O}r\textbf{d}inal \textbf{A}wareness), an Expectation-Maximization (EM)-based diffusion imputer built on the Elucidated Diffusion Model (EDM) framework. TabSODA propagates structural skips through the denoising loss and reverse-time sampler, and represents ordinal variables with cumulative-probit scalar latents while retaining analog-bit encodings for nominal variables. When a codebook skip mask is available, TabSODA uses it directly; otherwise, the TabSODA+SKIP variant estimates the mask from raw responses and questionnaire order using a CART-based skip-pattern miner. On Population Assessment of Tobacco and Health (PATH) study and the National Survey on Drug Use and Health (NSDUH), two nationally representative U.S.\ surveys, TabSODA reduces ordinal MACE by up to $23.7\%$ and improves categorical accuracy by up to $9\%$ over the strongest baseline across MCAR, MAR, and MNAR masking. The skip miner achieves near-perfect precision on both datasets, allowing TabSODA+SKIP to closely track the codebook-mask variant.

</details>


### [209] [Environment-Robust Representation Learning with Empirical Bayes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05365)
*Yuli Slavutsky,Matthew Shen,Bohan Wu,David M. Blei*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向多环境预测问题的贝叶斯方法，假设环境仅改变隐变量的分布而观测生成机制保持稳定，通过变分目标函数中的跨环境平衡项实现域泛化，在多个真实场景中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多环境预测问题中，不同环境（如不同医院、队列）的数据分布存在偏移，但底层的因果/生成机制保持稳定。现有方法未能充分利用这种“隐变量分布变化而条件生成机制不变”的结构假设，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 1) 构建贝叶斯模型，假设环境仅改变隐变量先验分布 p(z|e)，而似然 p(x|z) 和 p(y|z) 跨环境不变；2) 推导变分目标（ELBO），揭示其自然分解为各环境项与跨环境平衡项；3) 采用经验贝叶斯方法设置先验参数；4) 开发摊销变分推理算法进行后验近似，并利用学到的隐变量进行新环境预测。

Result: 在模拟实验以及天文源识别、微生物组疾病检测、ICU脓毒症预测三个真实世界研究中，该方法在新环境下的预测性能均优于现有域泛化方法。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模环境驱动的隐变量分布变化并引入跨环境平衡机制，该方法有效实现了分布外泛化，在多个领域中展示了其实用价值。

Abstract: We consider multi-environment prediction problems. We assume the environments change the distribution of a latent variable, while the mechanisms generating observed covariates and targets remain stable conditional on that variable. For example, hospitals or clinical cohorts may differ in the prevalence of latent patient states, even though the relationships between those states, physiological measurements, and outcomes remain unchanged. Given a dataset from multiple environments, we formulate a Bayesian model for such problems and derive the corresponding variational objective. We show that this objective decomposes into per-environment terms and an additional cross-environment balancing term induced by the model's structure. We use an empirical Bayes method to set the prior and incorporate it into the objective. Based on this objective, we develop an amortized variational algorithm for posterior approximation, and use the resulting learned latent variables to form predictions in new environments.We study our approach through simulations and real-world studies of astronomical source identification, microbiome-based disease detection, and ICU sepsis prediction. Across these settings, our method outperforms previous approaches for prediction in new environments.

</details>


### [210] [Sparse Functional Singular Value Decomposition for Biclustering and Triclustering Longitudinal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05488)
*Yue Zhao,Thierry Chekouo,Sandra Safo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: Tri-SfSVD 是一种统一的稀疏函数奇异值分解框架，通过在受试者、特征和时间子区域上施加稀疏惩罚，直接从不规则采样的纵向高维数据中发现双聚类和三聚类结构，在 IBD 多组学和脑电图数据中均取得了可解释的发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂疾病（如炎症性肠病）的亚型识别需要从纵向组学数据中捕获潜在模式，但这类数据通常具有高维度、稀疏采样和时间点不规则的特点，传统双聚类和函数数据分析方法难以有效处理。

Method: 提出 Tri-SfSVD 框架，将连续轨迹估计与受试者、特征和时间子区域的选择整合到统一优化问题中，通过施加稀疏惩罚直接在观测数据上发现局部结构，涵盖受试者层面、受试者-特征层面和受试者-特征-时间层面。

Result: 模拟实验表明 Tri-SfSVD 在高维场景下优于现有方法；在 IBD 多组学数据中识别出三个双聚类，关联了疾病亚型与微生物通路；在多通道脑电图数据中识别出三个三聚类，揭示了酒精相关表型与局部脑活动模式的关联及时间子区域差异。

Conclusion: Tri-SfSVD 为纵向高维数据的双/三聚类分析提供了灵活统一的框架，无需插补或形状同质性假设，能有效揭示复杂疾病的异质性结构。

Abstract: Identifying subtypes of complex conditions, such as Inflammatory Bowel Disease (IBD), often requires capturing latent patterns in longitudinal omics data. However, these data are typically high-dimensional, sparsely sampled, and irregularly observed over time, posing substantial challenges for conventional (bi)clustering and functional data analysis methods. We propose Tri-SfSVD, a unified sparse functional Singular Value Decomposition framework for discovering biclusters and triclusters in longitudinal data. Unlike existing functional biclustering methods that rely on ad hoc imputation or enforce restrictive shape-homogeneity assumptions, Tri-SfSVD integrates continuous trajectory estimation with simultaneous subject, feature, and temporal selection within a single optimization framework. By imposing sparse penalties across subjects, variables, and temporal subregions, the proposed method works directly on observed data to uncover localized structures at the subject, subject-feature, and subject-feature-time levels. Extensive simulations demonstrate that Tri-SfSVD outperforms existing approaches in high-dimensional settings. Applied to IBD multi-omics data, the method identified three biclusters linking sample clusters with distinct IBD-related clinical characteristics to microbial pathway groups associated with specific bacterial taxa, providing interpretable subject-pathway associations for characterizing disease heterogeneity. Applied to multi-channel EEG data, the method identified three triclusters linking sample clusters with distinct alcohol-related phenotypes to localized brain activity patterns, including subgroup differences separated by temporal subregions within the same spatial region.

</details>


### [211] [Conformal Risk-Averse Decision Making with Action Conditional Guarantee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05551)
*Zihan Zhu,Shayan Kiyani,George Pappas. Hamed Hassani*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文将共形预测从边缘安全保证推广到动作条件安全保证，提出动作条件共形预测，为风险规避决策者提供显式条件化的不确定性量化，并基于pinball损失最小化给出了有限样本算法，在真实数据集上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共形预测方法（如Kiyani et al., 2025b）虽能将预测集转化为最优风险规避决策策略，但仅提供边缘（marginal）安全保证，无法针对决策者采取的每个具体动作提供条件化的安全保证。实际决策场景中，决策者需要的是给定动作下的条件风险控制（如动作条件在险价值，action-conditional VaR），而非平均意义上的保证。

Method: （1）引入动作条件共形预测（action-conditional conformal prediction），使安全保证显式地条件化于决策者采取的每个动作；（2）证明动作条件预测集可作为风险规避决策者优化动作条件在险价值时的可行决策空间代理；（3）提出基于pinball损失最小化的有限样本算法，将Gibbs et al. (2025)的框架与动作条件保证相连接。

Result: 在两个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，所提方法在动作条件性能上相比共形预测基线有显著提升。

Conclusion: 动作条件共形预测成功将共形预测的安全保证从边缘层面推广到动作条件层面，为风险规避决策提供了更细粒度的不确定性量化工具，具有理论与实用双重价值。

Abstract: Reliable decision making pipelines powered by machine learning models require uncertainty quantification (UQ) methods that come with explicit safety guarantees. Conformal prediction provides such UQ by wrapping ML predictions into prediction sets, and recent work by Kiyani et al.
