(2025b) established that these sets can be translated into optimal risk-averse decision policies -- yet only inheriting marginal safety guarantees. We generalize and strengthen their results by (i) introducing action-conditional conformal prediction, which yields safety guarantees conditioned explicitly on each action taken by the decision maker, (ii) showing that action-conditional prediction sets serve as a proxy for the feasible decision space for risk-averse decision makers aiming to optimize action-conditional value-at-risk, and (iii) proposing a principled finite-sample algorithm based on pinball-loss minimization, connecting the framework of Gibbs et al. (2025) to action-conditional guarantees. Experiments on two real-world datasets confirm that our approach significantly improves action-conditional performance over conformal baselines.

</details>


### [212] [Finding Most Influential Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05919)
*Lucas D. Konrad,Nikolas Kuschnig*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种识别最具影响力集合（MIS）的高效算法，针对具有线性分式留出效应的估计目标，将原本不可行的组合搜索问题转化为一参数序列的top-k问题，利用Dinkelbach方法实现每次迭代O(n)成本且有限步终止，并在适当条件下保证全局最优性和精确集合恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别规模为k的最具影响力子集（MIS）——即移除后能最大程度改变目标估计量的子集——在计算上通常不可行，因为需要在C(n,k)个子集中进行穷举搜索。本文旨在解决这一计算瓶颈，使得MIS识别在实际数据规模下成为可能。

Method: 作者首先证明，对于具有线性分式留出效应的估计目标，MIS选择可归约为一个一参数序列的top-k问题。随后采用Dinkelbach方法进行求解，该算法每次迭代成本为O(n)，且具有有限步终止性质。对于固定残差化输入，算法返回单变量比率目标的全局最优集；当使用估计的冗余函数时，通过均匀分母和生成得分稳定性条件，保证对一阶正交得分目标的逼近，并在分离条件下实现精确的集合恢复。

Result: 在固定残差化输入下，算法能返回包括oracle-残差化部分线性模型在内的全局最优集。在估计冗余函数的情形下，均匀分母和生成得分稳定性确保了近似最优性，而分离条件则保证了精确的MIS恢复。模拟实验和实际应用表明，该方法能够恢复此前计算上不可达的精确MIS。

Conclusion: 该方法成功将MIS识别的组合爆炸问题转化为可高效求解的序列top-k问题，通过Dinkelbach算法实现了线性复杂度的迭代求解，在理论和实践上均验证了其有效性和最优性，显著扩展了影响力分析的计算可行性边界。

Abstract: Identifying most influential sets (MIS) - size-$k$ subsets whose removal maximally changes a target estimand - is typically infeasible because it requires searching over $\binom{n}{k}$ subsets. For estimands with linear-fractional leave-set-out effects, we show that MIS selection reduces to a one-parameter sequence of top-$k$ problems. Dinkelbach's method yields an algorithm with $\mathcal{O}(n)$ cost per iteration and finite termination. For fixed residualized inputs, the algorithm returns a globally optimal set for the univariate ratio objective, including the oracle-residualized partial linear model. With estimated nuisance functions, uniform denominator and generated-score stability imply approximation to the first-order oracle orthogonal-score objective; exact set recovery follows under a separation condition. Simulations and applications show that the method recovers exact MIS that were previously computationally inaccessible.

</details>


### [213] [EML-CD: Causal Mechanism Recovery via EML Symbolic Trees in Structure Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05942)
*Sota Asanuma*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: EML-CD将可解释的函数组合算子（EML）嵌入因果结构学习，在恢复DAG结构的同时，为每条边自动发现闭式因果方程，实现可解释的机制恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于神经网络的非线性因果发现方法虽然能恢复DAG结构，但每条边的因果机制仍是黑箱。Waxman等人指出从NN权重中提取因果机制本身是病态问题（ill-posed）。因此需要一种既能学习因果结构、又能为每条边提供可解释闭式方程的方法。

Method: 提出EML-CD框架，核心是将EML算子（能通过单一二元运算符组合出初等函数）融入因果结构学习。每条边机制表示为一棵门控EML二叉树（gated EML binary tree），自动搜索并发现闭式因果方程。分析雅可比矩阵可直接从输出方程计算，从而定量理解因果效应。支持深度扩展（如depth-2模型）。

Result: 在Sachs蛋白信号数据集（d=11）上，SHD=11.2±0.4（5种子平均），与PC/GES在种子方差内持平，优于CAM，同时为每条检测到的边附加闭式方程（精确率0.756，召回率0.365）。在受控双变量测试中，11个初等函数族中完整恢复了10个（保留形状相关性≥0.96），仅高频正弦部分恢复。在符号合成基准上，保留机制f-MSE（均值3.67）远优于固定SINDy字典（7644），但结构恢复（SHD 14.0）仅与字典持平，不及专用优化器。在Causal Chambers光隧道子集上，depth-2模型F1（0.444）优于线性OLS-BIC（0.273）。

Conclusion: EML-CD成功在因果结构学习中同步实现可解释的机制恢复，生成的闭式方程可通过分析雅可比矩阵直接量化因果效应。该方法在机制拟合精度上显著优于固定字典类方法，且避免了灾难性外推问题，但在纯结构恢复指标上仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Neural network (NN)-based nonlinear causal discovery methods recover DAG structure but leave each causal mechanism as a black box. Waxman et al. argued that extracting causal mechanisms from NN weights is ill-posed. We propose EML-CD, a framework that integrates the EML operator (capable of composing elementary functions from a single binary operator) into causal structure learning, with interpretable mechanism recovery as the primary objective. EML-CD represents each edge mechanism as a gated EML binary tree and automatically discovers closed-form causal equations. Analytical Jacobians can be directly computed from the output equations, enabling quantitative understanding of causal effects. On real data (Sachs protein signaling, d=11), EML-CD achieves SHD=11.2 +/- 0.4 (5-seed mean; baselines are single deterministic runs), on par with PC/GES within seed variance and below CAM, while attaching closed-form equations to each detected edge (precision 0.756, recall 0.365). In a controlled bivariate test with known mechanisms, EML-CD recovers 10 of 11 elementary function families faithfully (held-out shape correlation >= 0.96; only high-frequency sine is partial). On a symbolic synthetic benchmark, EML-CD attains a substantially lower and more stable held-out mechanism f-MSE than a fixed SINDy dictionary (mean 3.67 vs. 7644, the latter inflated by catastrophic extrapolation on one seed), although its structure recovery (SHD 14.0) only matches the dictionary and stays below specialized optimizers; on the Causal Chambers light-tunnel subset, a depth-2 model improves F1 over linear OLS-BIC (0.444 vs. 0.273).

</details>


### [214] [Fast and Robust Convergence Rate for TD(0) with Linear Function Approximation, Universal Learning Steps and I.I.D. Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05967)
*Ziad Kobeissi,Éloïse Berthier*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了带线性函数逼近的TD(0)算法的有限时间收敛行为，在i.i.d.采样和Polyak-Juditsky平均下，建立了均方误差(MSE)的O(1/k)收敛速率，该速率不依赖于特征协方差矩阵的最小特征值，从而对病态条件具有鲁棒性，且常数因子小于11。此外，提出了PCTD(0)变体，在强混合条件下具有更好的收敛性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TD(0)文献中的O(1/k)收敛速率均依赖于线性参数化下非中心协方差矩阵的最小特征值，这使得在特征矩阵病态时收敛保证变得脆弱。作者旨在建立一个不依赖该特征值、对病态条件鲁棒且常数因子明确且较小的快速收敛速率。

Method: 考虑on-policy的i.i.d.样本设定：采用常数学习步长（constant learning step）结合Polyak-Juditsky平均（即对迭代参数取时间平均）的TD(0)算法；通过精细的随机近似理论分析，导出均方误差上界；引入PCTD(0)变体，在马尔可夫链满足强混合条件的额外假设下改进收敛性质。

Result: 建立了TD(0)在均方误差意义上的新收敛速率：阶数为O(1/k)；仅依赖初始误差和模型无关常数，不依赖协方差矩阵最小特征值，对病态条件鲁棒；上界常数因子小于11，具有尖锐性（sharp）。此外，PCTD(0)在强混合条件下享有更好的收敛保证。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在TD(0)中获得了不依赖最小特征值的O(1/k)快速收敛速率，显著提升了对病态问题的鲁棒性，且常数因子明确且较小（<11），优于所有先前工作。PCTD(0)变体进一步拓展了在非i.i.d.马尔可夫采样下的优势。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the finite-time behavior of the TD(0) temporal-difference method with linear function approximation (LFA). We consider on-policy independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.) samples, a constant learning step, and the Polyak-Juditsky averaging method. We establish a new convergence rate, for the Mean-Square Error (MSE) on the approximated function, that is (i) fast in the sense that it admits an optimal dependency in the number of iterations k (i.e., of order 1/k), (ii) robust to ill-conditioning: it only depends on an initial error and modelindependent constants and (iii) sharp up to a multiplicative constant lower than 11. In particular, it does not depend on the smallest eigenvalue of the uncentered covariance matrix of the linear parametrization, unlike all pre-existing O(1/k) rates in the TD(0) literature. We also introduce PCTD(0), a variant of TD(0), which benefits from better convergence properties under an additional assumption of strong mixing on the Markov Chain.

</details>


### [215] [Adaptive Learning Rates with Surrogate Probability for Follow-the-Perturbed-Leader](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06043)
*Jongyeong Lee,Junya Honda,Shinji Ito,Chansoo Kim*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于替代概率函数的自适应学习率方法，用于FTPL框架，实现了对任意形状参数α>1的Pareto扰动的最佳双世界（BOBW）保证，并将该方法扩展到带有专家建议的赌博机问题中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FTRL框架中自适应学习率已取得BOBW保证，但其计算高效的替代方案FTPL由于无需优化的特性，使得设计依赖概率的自适应学习率变得困难。现有FTPL的BOBW保证仅限于特定α=2的情形，亟需一种通用的自适应学习率设计方法。

Method: 作者引入替代概率函数（surrogate probability functions），这些函数仅从可用量中计算，无需精确概率值。基于这些替代函数设计自适应学习率，为FTPL配备Pareto扰动（任意形状参数α>1），并将该方法推广至带有专家建议的赌博机问题中。

Result: 成功为任意α>1的Pareto扰动FTPL提供了BOBW保证，突破了先前仅限α=2的限制；同时在带专家建议的赌博机问题中也获得了BOBW保证。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持FTPL计算简洁性的同时实现了概率依赖的自适应能力，且基于替代函数的方法论可能对FTPL以外的算法框架和学习率设计具有独立的研究价值。

Abstract: Follow-the-regularized-leader framework has shown effectiveness and flexibility in online learning problems, where the choice of learning rates are known to be crucial. Recently, adaptive learning rates defined in terms of the arm-selection probabilities, obtained by solving convex optimization, have achieved improved best-of-both-worlds (BOBW) guarantees in various bandit problems. In contrast, BOBW guarantees for its computationally efficient alternative, follow-the-perturbed-leader (FTPL), remain relatively limited since its optimization-free nature ironically makes the design of adaptive, probability-dependent learning rates non-trivial. To address this challenge, we propose an adaptive learning rate for FTPL by introducing surrogate probability functions that can be computed only from the available quantities, without requiring the exact probabilities. Based on these learning rates with surrogate functions, we provide the BOBW guarantee for FTPL with Pareto perturbations for any shape parameter $α>1$, generalizing prior results restricted to specific choices of $α=2$. We further show the BOBW guarantees for FTPL with adaptive learning rates in the bandit problem with expert advices. Our approach preserves the computational simplicity of FTPL while enabling probability-dependent adaptivity, and the surrogate-based methodology may be of independent interest in other algorithmic frameworks beyond FTPL and learning rate designs.

</details>


### [216] [Effective Dimensionality as an Operator Invariant for Physics-Preserving Constraint Adaptation in Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06171)
*Cornelius Otchere,Michael Shields*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文利用Fisher信息矩阵分析物理信息神经网络（PINN）中物理方程与边界条件之间的任务冲突，提出有效自由度 $d_{eff}$ 作为结构不变量来量化微分算子未约束的参数方向。当 $d_{eff}$ 趋于零时，物理与边界约束完全吸收网络自由度。基于此，提出子空间投影策略，可在不破坏已学习物理的前提下快速适配新边界条件，仅需秒到分钟级时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN 因共享参数空间同时满足控制微分方程和边界条件而固有地存在任务干扰（task interference），需要对这一结构冲突进行定量分析和有效缓解。

Method: 1. 利用 Fisher 信息矩阵定义并计算物理约束模型的有效自由度 $d_{eff}$，度量微分算子未约束的参数方向维度；2. 分析有限维核与无限维核算子下 $d_{eff}$ 的收敛行为；3. 提出子空间投影策略：将参数更新投影到预训练物理算子的零空间中，实现边界条件适配而不干扰已学物理。

Result: 1. 对于有限维核算子，$d_{eff}$ 精确收敛到核维度，与网络宽度、深度、激活函数无关，成为连续算子的结构不变量；2. 对于无限维核算子，$d_{eff}$ 度量子空间的有限维表示带宽；3. 子空间投影策略在秒到分钟级即可实现与梯度微调相当甚至更优的边界适配质量，在线性与非线性算子上均验证有效。

Conclusion: $d_{eff}$ 从拟合诊断工具提升为微分算子的结构不变量，为零时标志着物理与边界约束完全约束网络。子空间投影为边界适配提供了高效替代方案，避免了从头重训练的高成本。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks inherently suffer from task interference because they rely on a shared parameter space to satisfy both governing differential equations and boundary conditions. We analyze this structural conflict using the Fisher Information Matrix to quantify the effective degrees of freedom ($d_{eff}$) in a physics-constrained model. Unlike the classical $d_{eff}$ which measures how many parameter directions are informed by data against a statistical prior, our $d_{eff}$ measures the dimension of the parameter directions unconstrained by the differential operator. For operators with finite-dimensional kernel, we show that $d_{eff}$ converges to the kernel dimension exactly, independent of network width, depth, or activation function, recasting it from a fit diagnostic into a structural invariant of the underlying continuous operator. For operators with infinite-dimensional kernel, $d_{eff}$ instead measures the network's finite-dimensional representational bandwidth for that kernel rather than recovering an integer invariant. Importantly, $d_{eff}$ also serves as an a priori structural diagnostic. Driving $d_{eff}$ of a well-posed problem to zero certifies that the physics and boundary constraints have absorbed the network's free directions. Building on this characterization, we introduce subspace projection strategies for boundary adaptation. Rather than retraining from scratch, we project parameter updates into the null space of the pre-trained physics operator so that new boundary conditions are satisfied without disturbing the learned physics. Gradient-based fine-tuning can match or exceed this but needs more wall-clock time and tuning, whereas subspace projection delivers near-equivalent quality in seconds to minutes. We validate on linear and nonlinear operators, demonstrating accurate adaptation to initial and boundary shifts and unencountered constraint types.

</details>


### [217] [Diffusion Models Observe Only Gradients: A Geometric Perspective on Score Matching Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06179)
*Naïl B. Khelifa,Richard E. Turner,Ramji Venkataramanan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 扩散模型的L2分数匹配误差并非衡量边际分布质量的合适内在度量：模型可完美匹配目标分布却产生任意大的L2分数误差。通过亥姆霍兹-霍奇分解，作者揭示了只有梯度分量进入边际Fokker-Planck动力学，而螺线管分量在结构上不可见。基于此，他们证明了L2分数误差的单调函数无法一致下界任何分布散度（不可能性结果），推导出仅依赖可观测梯度分量的KL散度上界（收紧Girsanov界），并给出梯度分量的可计算估计器，其与样本质量的实证相关性显著优于全L2误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示标准L2分数匹配误差作为扩散模型训练目标和理论分析工具的根本性缺陷：L2分数误差与边际分布质量之间存在结构性脱节，需要找到真正决定采样质量的内在误差度量。

Method: 利用亥姆霍兹-霍奇分解将分数误差分解为梯度分量（curl-free）和螺线管分量（divergence-free）；分析Fokker-Planck方程的边际动力学，证明仅梯度分量影响分布演化；通过Girsanov定理和测度变换推导KL散度上界；利用对偶Sobolev恒等式构造梯度分量的可处理估计器。

Result: （1）不可能性定理：任何L2分数误差的单调函数无法一致下界学习分布与目标分布间的散度；（2）KL散度的新上界仅依赖梯度分量，比标准Girsanov界更紧，揭示其松弛源于在路径空间而非边际空间操作；（3）梯度分量估计器与样本质量（如FID）的Spearman秩相关性（约0.8）显著优于全L2误差（约0.3）。

Conclusion: L2分数误差不应作为扩散模型的唯一评估指标；梯度分量才是决定边际分布质量的核心量；所提出的梯度分量估计器为模型选择、早停等实际任务提供了更可靠的信号，呼吁重新审视扩散模型的理论基础与评估实践。

Abstract: Score-based diffusion models are typically trained by minimizing the $L^2$ score matching error, and standard theoretical analyses rely on this quantity to bound the sampling discrepancy between the learned and target distributions. We show the $L^2$ score error is not the right intrinsic measure of marginal distributional quality: a learned diffusion model can incur arbitrarily large $L^2$ score error while perfectly matching the target distribution. By decomposing score errors into a gradient and a solenoidal component (a Helmholtz-Hodge decomposition), we identify the geometric reason behind this: only the gradient component enters the marginal Fokker-Planck dynamics, while the solenoidal component is structurally invisible. We make this precise in three results. First, building on the corrected geometry, we prove an impossibility result: no monotone function of the $L^2$ score error can uniformly lower bound any divergence between the learned and target distributions. Second, we derive an upper bound on the Kullback-Leibler divergence that depends only on the observable gradient component of the error, tightening the standard Girsanov bound and identifying its looseness as the cost of operating on path-space rather than marginal-space dynamics. Third, we give a tractable estimator of the gradient component via a dual Sobolev identity, which is shown to empirically correlate substantially better with sample quality than the full $L^2$ error.

</details>


### [218] [Anchor PCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06233)
*Benedikt Seiter,Anya Fries,Julius von Kügelgen,Jonas Peters*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: Anchor PCA 是一种面向多域数据的鲁棒无监督降维方法，通过在整体解释方差与共享/域特定低秩嵌入之间权衡，利用修改后的目标矩阵高效求解，能恢复最大不变子空间，并在未见域上优于池化PCA和 worst-case 替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统池化PCA处理多域数据时容易捕捉仅少数域中变异大的虚假方向，导致嵌入在未见域上泛化性差。需要一种能聚焦于跨域共享变异方向的鲁棒降维方法。

Method: 提出 Anchor PCA，在目标函数中权衡总体解释方差与共享嵌入和域特定嵌入之间的一致性；等价于对修正目标矩阵执行标准PCA，可高效求解；理论上证明其恢复最大不变子空间，并在有界域特定协方差膨胀下具有极小极大重构解释。

Result: 在模拟数据和含时间漂移的真实气体传感器数据上，Anchor PCA 恢复了最大不变子空间，且在未见域上解释的方差显著优于池化PCA基线及 worst-case 方法。

Conclusion: Anchor PCA 是多域数据鲁棒无监督降维的一种有前景的方法。

Abstract: Principal component analysis (PCA) is one of the most widely used unsupervised dimension reduction techniques. We study PCA for data from multiple related domains. Since principal components generally differ across domains, one way to obtain a shared low-rank embedding is to perform PCA on the pooled data. However, this approach can focus on spurious directions that exhibit high variation in only a few domains. To find a robust embedding that still explains most variance in unseen but similar domains, we propose instead to focus on shared directions of variation. To this end, we introduce Anchor PCA which trades off overall explained variance with agreement between the shared and domain-specific low-rank embeddings. Anchor PCA amounts to PCA on a modified target matrix and thus can be solved efficiently. Moreover, we show that Anchor PCA recovers a maximal invariant subspace and admits a minimax reconstruction interpretation under bounded domain-specific covariance inflations. On simulated and real-world gas sensor data with temporal drift, we demonstrate, respectively, that Anchor PCA recovers the maximally invariant subspace and yields embeddings that explain more variance on unseen domains than the pooling baseline and a worst-case alternative. Taken together, these findings establish Anchor PCA as a promising approach to robust unsupervised dimension reduction from multi-domain data.

</details>


### [219] [Discrete Causal Representations from Heterogeneous Domains: A Bayesian Approach with Social Survey Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06288)
*Ankur Garg,Michael Stettler,Aaron Schein,Julius von Kügelgen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出一种贝叶斯方法，从多环境离散数据中学习因果表征，通过序贯蒙特卡洛采样近似多模态后验，并在社会调查数据上验证了其推断潜在因果概念及其关系的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果表征学习研究多聚焦于可识别性理论，缺乏实用的不确定性感知方法及真实世界应用探索，尤其在离散因果概念与未知多节点软干预场景下存在空白。

Method: 构建层次贝叶斯模型，将因果假设和可解释性需求转化为适当的先验分布与参数化选择；设计基于序贯蒙特卡洛（SMC）采样的推断方案，以近似多模态后验分布。

Result: 在社会调查数据的案例研究中，模型能够推断出有意义的高层概念（如文化价值观、政治观点）及其之间的合理因果关系，其中潜在概念对应调查响应，环境对应不同国家或州。

Conclusion: 该方法为从复杂真实世界数据中学习因果表征提供了实用工具，展示了贝叶斯框架在多环境、离散概念和未知软干预场景下的有效性与应用潜力。

Abstract: Causal representation learning aims to infer the high-level latent causal concepts that give rise to observed low-level measurements. This is particularly relevant for heterogeneous data from different environments or domains since distribution shifts often arise through sparse, localized changes in some of the underlying causal mechanisms, while other parts of the generative process remain unchanged. Whereas identifiability of causal representations has been studied extensively, practical uncertainty-aware methods and real-world use cases remain less explored. In this work, we propose a Bayesian approach to learning causal representations from multi-environment data, focusing on the case of discrete causal concepts and unknown multi-node soft interventions. To this end, we translate causal assumptions and interpretability desiderata into suitable priors and parametric choices within a hierarchical model. We then devise an inference scheme based on sequential Monte Carlo sampling to approximate the resulting multimodal posterior. We showcase our approach through case studies on social survey data, where latent causal concepts correspond to cultural values or political opinions, measurements to survey responses, and environments to different countries or states. Our model infers meaningful high-level concepts and plausible causal relations among them, demonstrating its utility for learning causal representations of complex real-world data.

</details>


### [220] [Symmetric Divergence and Normalized Similarity: A Unified Topological Framework for Representation Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06342)
*Yan Wang,Tianyang Hu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套统一的拓扑数据分析工具包，包含对称表示拓扑散度（SRTD）和归一化拓扑相似度（NTS），分别用于细粒度结构诊断和跨场景鲁棒评估，解决了现有拓扑散度的不对称性和尺度依赖问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有配对的拓扑散度（如RTD）存在两个关键缺陷：一是启发式的不对称性导致理论不完备，二是分数无界且依赖于样本量，难以实现跨场景的可靠基准测试。这限制了TDA在神经网络表示比较中的实际应用。

Method: 作者提出两个互补工具：(1) SRTD及其高效变体SRTD-lite，通过对称化RTD框架，将诊断信息整合为单一的跨条形码签名，实现结构差异的精确定位，且无需双向计算；(2) NTS，通过测量层次合并顺序的秩相关性，得到一个限定在[-1,1]之间的尺度不变度量，克服了未归一化散度的尺度和样本依赖性。

Result: 在合成和真实深度学习场景的实验表明，该工具包能捕捉CNN中被几何度量忽略的功能性转变，并在距离饱和情况下仍能鲁棒地映射LLM的谱系关系。

Conclusion: 所提出的拓扑工具包为神经表示比较提供了严谨的拓扑感知视角，有效补充了CKA等现有几何度量，在细粒度诊断和标准化评估两个层面均取得了重要进展。

Abstract: Topological Data Analysis (TDA) offers a principled, intrinsic lens for comparing neural representations. However, existing paired topological divergences (e.g., RTD) are limited by heuristic asymmetry and, more critically, unbounded scores that depend on sample size, hindering reliable cross-scenario benchmarking. To address these challenges, we develop a unified topological toolkit serving two complementary needs: fine-grained structural diagnosis and robust, standardized evaluation. First, we complete the RTD framework by introducing Symmetric Representation Topology Divergence (SRTD) and its efficient variant SRTD-lite. Beyond resolving the theoretical asymmetry of prior variants, SRTD consolidates diagnostic information into a single, comprehensive cross-barcode signature. This allows for precise localization of structural discrepancies and serves as an effective optimization objective without the overhead of dual directional computations. Second, to enable reliable benchmarking across heterogeneous settings, we propose Normalized Topological Similarity (NTS). By measuring the rank correlation of hierarchical merge orders, NTS yields a scale-invariant metric bounded between -1 and 1, effectively overcoming the scale and sample-dependence of unnormalized divergences. Experiments across synthetic and real-world deep learning settings demonstrate that our toolkit captures functional shifts in CNNs missed by geometric measures and robustly maps LLM genealogy even under distance saturation, offering a rigorous, topology-aware perspective that complements measures like CKA.

</details>


### [221] [Function-Space Priors for Bayesian Neural ODEs with Application to Vessel Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06351)
*Jaeyeong Lee,Wonmo Koo,Heeyoung Kim*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向AIS船舶轨迹预测的贝叶斯神经常微分方程方法，通过在向量场上施加高斯过程核先验并结合概率多重打靶法，解决了标准贝叶斯Neural ODE中各向同性高斯权重先验无法编码船舶动力学结构信息的问题，实现了连续时间轨迹建模与可靠的预测不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AIS轨迹预测面临不规则采样、数据缺失和复杂动态等挑战，实际应用不仅需要准确的点预测，还需要良好校准的不确定性估计。贝叶斯Neural ODE提供了连续时间建模与不确定性量化的统一框架，但常用的各向同性高斯权重先验无法编码船舶动力学的平滑性和局部性等结构特性。现有函数空间贝叶斯神经网络方法仅适用于静态映射，无法直接迁移到以轨迹（而非向量场本身）为核心关注量的Neural ODE场景。理论上可在ODE解上直接放置高斯过程先验，但通过非线性ODE求解器传播分布在解析上不可行。

Method: 本文采用一种实用的折中方案：在有限测量点集上评估的向量场上直接施加基于GP核的先验。具体做法是在标准权重空间变分目标函数中增广一个基于核的正则化项，惩罚向量场偏离GP先验所隐含的结构。此外，为处理长序列和不规则采样的AIS轨迹，将函数空间正则化与概率多重打靶法相结合，在保持全局一致性的同时实现跨时间段的推断解耦。

Result: 摘要中未提供具体实验数值结果，需查阅正文获取定量评估数据。

Conclusion: 通过将GP核先验与概率多重打靶法引入贝叶斯Neural ODE框架，该方法能够有效建模船舶轨迹的连续时间动力学，并提供良好校准的不确定性估计，优于标准各向同性先验方案。

Abstract: Vessel trajectory prediction from Automatic Identification System (AIS) data is essential for maritime situational awareness, yet it remains challenging due to irregular sampling, missing reports, and complex dynamics. Beyond accurate point forecasts, maritime applications also demand well-calibrated uncertainty estimates for reliable decision-making. Bayesian Neural Ordinary Differential Equations (ODEs) offer a principled framework for continuous-time trajectory modeling with uncertainty quantification by placing a prior over the neural vector field parameters. However, the commonly used isotropic Gaussian weight prior fails to encode informative structural properties of vessel dynamics, such as smoothness and locality. Existing function-space Bayesian neural network methods address this limitation for static mappings, but do not transfer directly to Neural ODEs, where the primary quantity of interest is the trajectory rather than the vector field itself. In principle, one could place a Gaussian process (GP) prior directly over ODE solutions, but this requires propagating distributions through a nonlinear ODE solver, which is analytically intractable. To address this challenge, we adopt a practical approach that imposes a GP-kernel-based prior directly on the vector field evaluated at a finite set of measurement points. Specifically, we augment the standard weight-space variational objective with a kernel-based regularizer that penalizes deviations of the vector field from the structure implied by a GP prior. To handle long and irregular AIS trajectories, we further combine this function-space regularization with probabilistic multiple shooting, which decouples inference across temporal segments while maintaining global consistency.

</details>


### [222] [Conformal Risk Sharing: Certified Cost Allocation with Participation Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06391)
*Ieva Kazlauskaite*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出“认证分配问题”，设计共形风险分担框架，通过可解释的共享策略与分裂共形校准相结合，在无分布假设下为每个参与者提供义务上限保证，显著降低高风险个体的极端负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 罕见不良事件的财务冲击若由群体分担可缓解个体极端负担，但任何因此受损的参与者都有动机退出。因此需要一种可信机制，既能给每个参与者提供未来义务的可信上限，又需确保群体总体损害可控。

Method: 将问题形式化为“认证分配问题”（Certified Allocation Problem）。提出共形风险分担（Conformal Risk Sharing），将可解释的共享策略与分裂共形校准（split conformal calibration）相结合：在训练数据上调谐共享强度，利用留出的校准数据为每个参与者生成免分布、基于可交换性有效的个体保证。

Result: 在合成数据及真实世界数据（包括降水量数据和能源合作社数据）上的实验表明，该框架能显著降低高风险代理的极端义务，同时将对其他参与者的损害控制在可接受范围内。

Conclusion: 共形风险分担框架在无分布假设条件下，实现了可信、可验证的风险共享，为保险、互助组织等场景提供了兼具公平性与激励相容性的实用方案。

Abstract: Sharing the financial impact of rare adverse events across a group can soften extreme individual burdens, but any participant made worse off by the arrangement has reason to leave. A credible mechanism must therefore provide each agent with a trustworthy cap on their future obligation and should be deployed only if the aggregate harm across participants is bounded. We formalise this as the Certified Allocation Problem: from finite data and without distributional assumptions, find a redistribution rule, produce obligation caps for every participant, and verify that no participant is made materially worse off. We propose Conformal Risk Sharing, which solves this problem by pairing an interpretable sharing policy with split conformal calibration. The sharing intensity is tuned on training data, while held-out calibration data produces distribution-free per-agent guarantees (valid under exchangeability). Experiments on synthetic and real-world data, including precipitation and energy-cooperative data, confirm that the framework can substantially reduce extreme obligations for high-risk agents while controlling harm to others.

</details>


### [223] [Central Description Length (CDL) Clustering Validation Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05230)
*Mahdi Shamsi,Soosan Beheshti*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: CDL-CVI 是一种新的聚类验证指标，利用簇内紧凑性、估计中心和协方差，计算真实簇中心描述长度的概率上界，无需标签即可有效评估非凸、不规则形状的聚类结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流聚类验证指标（CVIs）大多基于欧氏紧凑性和分离性，倾向于偏好凸的、紧凑的划分，在非凸、不规则或变密度数据上性能退化。而使用核变换或替代距离度量的方法又引入额外的调参和计算成本，因此需要一种无需额外预处理即可有效评估任意形状聚类的新指标。

Method: 提出 Central Description Length (CDL) 聚类验证指标。CDL 利用可观测的簇内紧凑性、估计的簇中心和估计的簇协方差，计算与不可观测的真实簇中心相关联的描述长度的概率上界。该上界将簇内紧凑性和中心位移压缩为单一可计算量，仅依赖数据、划分、估计中心和协方差这些可观测量，无需真实标签。

Result: 在非凸和任意形状的合成基准上，CDL-CVI 比传统 CVI 更频繁地选出参考聚类数，并获得更高的调整兰德指数（ARI），且无需额外的核预处理。在基于冻结无监督嵌入聚类的图像基准（MNIST、CIFAR-10、STL-10）上，CDL-CVI 在 K-means、DBSCAN 和谱聚类中返回的聚类数均接近真实类别数。

Conclusion: CDL-CVI 提供了一种统一的方法来评估任意聚类算法产生的划分，在非凸和不规则形状数据上表现优于传统 CVI，并扩展了无需核预处理的适用场景。

Abstract: Selecting a clustering algorithm and its hyperparameters without labels is a common difficulty in engineering machine learning pipelines that work with unsupervised analysis of sensor, image, or process data. Clustering validation indices (CVIs) provide internal scores for ranking candidate clusterings, but most popular CVIs are built from Euclidean compactness and separation terms and so tend to favour compact, convex partitions. Their performance is known to degrade on non convex, irregular, or variable density data, where kernel transformations or alternative distance measures are typically used at the cost of additional tuning and computation. This paper introduces the Central Description Length (CDL) clustering validation index. CDL uses the observed within cluster compactness, the estimated cluster centers, and the estimated cluster covariances to compute a probabilistic upper bound on the description length associated with the unobservable true cluster centers. The bound condenses intra cluster compactness and centroid displacement into a single computable quantity and is evaluated on the partition produced by any clustering algorithm. The implementation uses only observable quantities (the data, the partition, the estimated centers, and the estimated covariances) and does not use ground truth labels. On synthetic benchmarks with non convex and arbitrary shape clusters, CDL-CVI selected the reference number of clusters more often and reached higher Adjusted Rand Index (ARI) values than the conventional CVIs we tested, without an additional kernel preprocessing stage. On image benchmarks (MNIST, CIFAR-10, STL-10) clustered from frozen unsupervised embeddings, CDL-CVI returned cluster numbers close to the reference class counts across K-means, DBSCAN, and spectral clustering in the reported trials.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [224] [The Evaluation Blind Spot: A Stereological Theory of Benchmark Coverage for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05169)
*Jason Z Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了LLM基准覆盖的立体度量理论，证明可见Hausdorff距离的上下界均涉及有效维度d_eff。实证发现三个主流榜单的d_eff在2.86–4.80之间，结构性盲区远超统计噪声。通过次模贪心算法找到稳定核心基准集，并解决了Gardner问题1.5的最小最大速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM评测榜单存在严重的结构性盲区：两个在已有基准上得分相同的模型，其真实能力轮廓之间可能存在巨大差异。传统方法缺乏从几何和统计角度量化基准套件"覆盖完备性"的理论框架，导致研究者无法判断排行榜排名在多大程度上能反映模型的真实能力排序，也无法系统性地选择最小且最优的基准子集。

Method: 本文方法分为三个层面：(1) 将能力轮廓建模为凸体，定义有效维度d_eff来刻画基准套件的实际自由度，推导可见Hausdorff距离的上下界；(2) 在三个独立榜单上估计d_eff，并计算结构性盲区与统计噪声的比值；(3) 使用卡方投影模型模拟不同隐能力先验下的排名交换率；(4) 采用Nemhauser (1-1/e)保证的次模贪心算法选择最小覆盖基准子集；(5) 通过最优恢复理论在球面S^(D-1)上解决Gardner问题1.5，建立C²支撑函数的极小极大速率Θ(R/(κ m^(2/(D-1))))。

Result: (1) 三个榜单的d_eff∈[2.86, 4.80]，结构性盲区超亚军差距两个数量级，主导统计噪声52-127倍；(2) 前两名模型的半分裂交换率在[0.38, 0.49]之间，500次随机可见/隐藏拆分中92%的试验交换了top-1排名，平均2.83/5个top-5模型变动；(3) 次模贪心找到4个基准的稳定核心，7/12个基准即可达到90%覆盖，子集跨季度迁移保留率93-97%；(4) 特征结构预测不可替代评估(ρ=-0.69, p=0.013)和外部评估信息增益(ρ=+0.38)均显著；(5) Gardner问题1.5在一般维度下获得紧的极小极大速率。

Conclusion: LLM基准评测存在根本性的几何约束：有效维度极低(d_eff<5)，意味着可见分数一致时隐藏能力差异巨大，排名高度不稳定。12个基准中仅需7个即可达到90%覆盖，且核心子集具有跨时间稳定性。Gardner问题1.5的解决为凸体恢复提供了独立的理论基础。

Abstract: We give a stereological theory of LLM benchmark coverage. For any suite with effective dimensionality d_eff, the visible Hausdorff distance between two convex capability profiles consistent with the same scores is bounded by epsilon + C R m^(-1/(d_eff-1)), with matching Lipschitz lower bound. Empirically, three independent leaderboards (Open LLM v2, an extended 12-benchmark suite, LiveBench) all have d_eff in [2.86, 4.80] on their competitive frontier; the structural blind spot exceeds the observed runner-up score gap by two orders of magnitude and dominates statistical noise by 52-127x. Under a chi-squared projection model, the isotropic prior is the optimistic case; across six hidden-capability priors and four ambient dimensions the simulated half-split swap rate of the top two models stays in [0.38, 0.49], and a 500-trial random visible/held-out split shows that 92% of trials swap the top-1 ranking with on average 2.83 of 5 top-5 models changing. A submodular greedy algorithm with the Nemhauser (1 - 1/e) guarantee finds a stable core of 4 benchmarks; 7 of 12 suffice for 90% coverage, and the trained subset transfers across temporal quarters with 93-97% retention. A counterfactual validation across 12 internal benchmarks and 27 Chatbot Arena categories confirms that the eigenstructure predicts which evaluations are irreplaceable (rho = -0.69, p = 0.013 for removal disruption) and which external evaluations bring new information (rho = +0.38). As a second, independent theoretical contribution, we resolve Gardner's Problem 1.5 (1995) for C^2 support functions, establishing the minimax rate Theta(R/(kappa m^(2/(D-1)))) in general dimension via optimal recovery theory on S^(D-1).

</details>


### [225] [ERRORQUAKE: Heavy-Tailed Error Severity Distributions in Open-Weight Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05170)
*Jason Z Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Errorquake-10k 基准和严重性分布分析方法，揭示了在匹配准确率下不同开源 LLM 的错误严重性分布存在显著差异。作者引入类似地震学中的 Gutenberg-Richter 分布指数 b 来刻画模型错误严重性的尾部分布，证明严重性剖面与错误率在信息论上不可相互约简，并发现错误类型随严重性等级发生类别性转变。他们主张严重性分布应与准确率并列为标准评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前幻觉基准测试仅报告单一错误计数，将所有错误视为等价——然而错误的日期与虚构的法庭裁决在严重程度上相差数个数量级。标量错误率掩盖了模型间错误严重性分布的结构性差异，使得在匹配准确率的模型之间无法做出有意义的区分。作者旨在揭示这一被忽视的维度，并提供量化工具。

Method: 构建 Errorquake-10k：包含10,000个查询的基准，覆盖8个领域和5个难度层级，每个回答在0-4连续严重性量表上评分。对21个开源模型拟合各自的严重性分布，计算 Gutenberg-Richter 上尾斜率 b 及其95% bootstrap 置信区间。进行519项三人评分的人类验证研究以确认测量可靠性（ICC=0.85）和 LLM-judge 排名效度（ρ=0.89）。提出不可约简定理，证明严重性剖面与错误率在信息论上不可相互替代（条件互信息 I(b; model | ε) = 1.56 bits，跨模型 b 方差的64.5%无法由 ε 解释）。构建严重性机制分类法（κ=0.83），分析不同严重性等级下的错误类型构成。

Result: 210个模型对中，85对在匹配准确率（|Δε| < 0.05）下具有不相交的95% b 置信区间（例如 deepseek-v3.2 vs ministral-14b：ε=0.586，Δb=0.47）。人类验证 ICC(2,k=3)=0.85，LLM-judge 与人类排名相关性 ρ=0.89，稠密模型规模与人类数据上的严重性呈负相关（ρ_s=-0.86）。信息论分析：I(b; model | ε)=1.56 bits，64.5%的 b 方差独立于 ε。严重性机制层面：低严重性错误以检索失败为主（71%），高严重性错误以虚构捏造为主（39%），且该构成在不同模型规模间差异显著（p<0.0001）。

Conclusion: 严重性分布承载了错误率无法捕捉的判别性信息，应作为标准评估指标与准确率并列报告。错误严重性维度揭示了模型行为中此前不可见的系统性差异——错误类型随严重性等级的类别性转变以及模型规模对这一构成的显著影响，为理解幻觉机制和模型改进提供了新视角。

Abstract: At matched accuracy, open-weight LLMs differ substantially in the shape of their error severity distribution -- a difference invisible to the scalar error rate. Hallucination benchmarks report a single error count and treat all errors as equivalent, yet a wrong date and a fabricated court ruling differ by orders of magnitude. We introduce Errorquake-10k, a 10,000-query benchmark scoring each response on a continuous 0-4 severity scale across 8 domains and 5 difficulty tiers, and we fit per-model severity distributions for 21 open-weight models. For each model we estimate a severity distribution index (b, the Gutenberg-Richter upper-tail slope) with 95% bootstrap confidence intervals. Headline: across the 210 model pairs, 85 have disjoint 95% b confidence intervals at matched accuracy (|Delta epsilon| < 0.05) on human-consensus scoring, e.g. deepseek-v3.2 vs. ministral-14b at epsilon = 0.586 and Delta b = 0.47. A 519-item three-rater human validation study confirms measurement reliability (ICC(2,k=3) = 0.85), validates the LLM-judge ranking (rho = 0.89), and confirms the dense-model scaling correlation on human data (rho_s = -0.86). We prove a Non-Reducibility Theorem showing that severity profile and error rate are informationally non-redundant (I(b; model | epsilon) = 1.56 bits; 64.5% of cross-model b variance is unexplained by epsilon). A severity mechanism taxonomy (kappa = 0.83) reveals that error type shifts categorically with severity: low-severity errors are retrievals (71%); high-severity errors are fabrications (39%) -- and this composition differs by model size (p < 0.0001). Severity distribution should be reported alongside accuracy; it carries discriminative information that the error rate cannot.

</details>


### [226] [Staged Factorial Screening for Budget-Constrained Micro-Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05186)
*Felipe Chavarro Polania*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在预算受限的微预训练场景中，采用分阶段的部分因子实验设计工作流，能否在短时间预算下稳定地恢复早期效应结构。通过613次实验，结果表明短期设计筛选可识别高惩罚方向，并支持以"桥接"锚点为中心、在缩减空间内局部优化的推荐策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预算受限的微预训练通常需要在共享加速器上快速筛选大量候选配方（recipes），再进行大规模搜索。现有方法缺乏对短时间预算下效应结构稳定性的系统研究，作者希望验证分阶段的部分因子工作流能否有效且稳定地识别关键超参数效应。

Method: 采用固定自研单GPU训练循环，在2、5、10分钟预算下开展613次实验，包括：先导与跟进筛选（pilot and follow-up screens）；5和10分钟预算下完整的16条件种子重跑；定向种子锚点检查；同主机贪心搜索与成本匹配随机搜索基线；60分钟桥接实验包；以及在Windows A100和Linux L40S上的有界24小时锚点延续实验。使用Benjamini-Hochberg校正进行多重比较控制。

Result: 总批次大小、深度和宽度的惩罚效应在短预算时最大，随预算增加而减弱。在预注册的完整筛选家族中，D、A、B、C在5和10分钟预算下经BH校正后仍保持非零估计，而E未通过。随机搜索能找到强候选，但反复落在同一低惩罚区域且缺乏因子归因能力。60分钟桥接锚点均值最低，但无法区分工作流优化与更大模型容量优势。在12和24小时有界延续中，桥接锚点样本均值最低，非桥接排序则依赖主机环境。

Conclusion: 提出一个有界方法结论：使用短时设计筛选识别高惩罚方向，通过重复运行确认有前景的锚点，然后在缩减后的空间内进行局部优化。证据支持以桥接为中心的推荐策略（跨两种主机、24小时内有效），但不支持硬件无关的排名或通用的超参数优化优越性声明。

Abstract: Budget-constrained micro-pretraining often requires triaging many candidate recipes on a shared accelerator before larger search budgets are spent. We study whether a staged fractional-factorial workflow can recover stable early effect structure in this setting. On a fixed autoresearch-derived single-GPU training loop, we run 613 experiments across pilot and follow-up screens at 2, 5, and 10 minutes; full 16-condition seeded reruns at 5 and 10 minutes; targeted seeded anchor checks; same-host greedy and matched-cost random baselines; a 60-minute bridge package; and bounded Windows A100 and Linux L40S anchor continuations through 24 hours. Main penalties from total batch, depth, and width are largest at short budgets and relax as budget increases. Within the predeclared seeded full-screen families, D, A, B, and C retain non-zero estimates at 5 and 10 minutes after within-budget Benjamini-Hochberg correction, while E does not. Random search can reach strong incumbents in this 32-condition space, but repeatedly in the same low-penalty region and without factor attribution. The 60-minute bridge anchor has the lowest mean, although that package does not separate workflow refinement from the larger bridge model's capacity advantage. In bounded 12-hour and 24-hour three-anchor continuations on both hosts, the bridge has the lowest sample mean while the non-bridge ordering stays host-sensitive. We therefore present a bounded methods result: use short designed screens to identify high-penalty directions, confirm promising anchors under repeated runs, and refine locally inside the reduced space. The evidence supports a bridge-centered recommendation through 24 hours on two hosts, not hardware-invariant ranking or general hyperparameter-optimization superiority.

</details>


### [227] [PyCC.id: A package for hypothesis-driven equation discovery with structural identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05191)
*Federico J. Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PyCC是一个Python库，它通过骨架（skeleton）驱动的方法，结合特征曲线（CCs）灵感的结构化先验，从时序数据中发现常微分方程（ODE），以缓解逆问题的病态性并避免后验人工筛选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动方程发现本质上是一个病态逆问题，常产生多个拟合效果相近的候选模型。现有方法虽可通过先验约束缩小搜索空间，但仍需依赖领域专家的后验人工筛选，缺乏一种系统化、可迭代、具备结构可辨识性保证的框架。

Method: 提出基于特征曲线（CCs）启发的“骨架”方法论：用户定义与某族ODE关联的结构骨架，迭代融入领域假设与先验进行模型精化。骨架具有可证明的结构可辨识性，可用于检验骨架正确性。该方法具有模块化特点，兼容神经网络、符号回归、稀疏回归等多种方程发现范式，并实现为Python库PyCC。

Result: 开发了PyCC库，将骨架驱动的方程发现流程整合为灵活工具，使研究人员和工程师能够无缝定义骨架与假设，从时序数据中发现ODE。

Conclusion: PyCC通过骨架驱动的假设融入和结构可辨识性检查，为数据驱动ODE发现提供了一种更系统、可解释且无需后验人工筛选的解决方案，其模块化设计使其能适配多种底层算法范式。

Abstract: Data-driven equation discovery is fundamentally an inverse problem that seeks to infer the governing differential equations of a system directly from time-series measurements. A known issue is the ill-conditioned nature of the inverse problem, which frequently produces multiple mathematical models that fit the data similarly well. One path to address this issue is by incorporating known hypotheses and constraints into the training phase beforehand. While this approach effectively reduces the search space, it still results in multiple candidate models, forcing practitioners to rely on post-hoc manual filtering based on their own domain expertise. A recent approach incorporates structural `skeletons' inspired by characteristic curves (CCs), defining a hypothesis-driven methodology. In this methodology, practitioners define a skeleton, which is associated with a family of ordinary differential equations (ODEs), and then add their hypotheses and priors based on their domain knowledge to refine the obtained model iteratively. An important advantage of this approach is that some skeletons have demonstrable structural identifiability properties, which are useful for checking whether the skeleton is correct or should be discarded. Furthermore, this formalism enables the use of multiple equation discovery paradigms due to its modularity (such as neural networks, symbolic regression, and sparse regression). In this work, we present the Python library PyCC, which condenses these efforts into a flexible tool that allows researchers and engineers to seamlessly define their skeletons and hypotheses to discover ODEs from time-dependent data.

</details>


### [228] [Temporal Preference Concepts and their Functions in a Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05194)
*Ian Rios-Sialer,Shantanu Darveshi,Shuai Jiang,Avigya Paudel,Anastasiia Pronina,Ipshita Bandyopadhyay,Justin Shenk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过因果定位方法，在蒸馏大语言模型Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507中识别出编码时间偏好的中层到上层节点子图，发现模型对未来贴现的陡峭程度远低于人类但不稳定，并初步证明操控向量可改变时间偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs越来越多地被用于需要权衡短期收益与长期后果的决策场景，但对其内部如何表征和解决这种时间权衡知之甚少。现有工作缺乏对时间偏好神经机制的因果理解，无法实现可靠的外部控制。本研究旨在填补这一空白，为实现对LLM规划与推理的可靠控制奠定基础。

Method: 以蒸馏模型Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507为对象，采用梯度归因和激活修补（activation patching）两种互补方法进行因果定位，收敛地识别出编码时间偏好的中层到上层节点子图；分析残差流中时间视野的几何编码；通过行为实验比较LLM与人类的时间贴现差异及跨情境稳定性；初步探索操控向量（steering vectors）对时间偏好的调控效果。

Result: 成功定位到中层到上层transformer层中负责时间偏好的节点子图；时间视野的几何信息编码在预期定位层的残差流中；未干预的LLM对未来贴现的陡峭程度比人类低数倍，但该偏好跨情境不稳定；操控向量可初步实现时间偏好的方向性改变。

Conclusion: 机制可解释性方法能有效定位LLM中的时间偏好子图，揭示了模型与人类在跨期决策上的显著差异及其不稳定性，为通过操控向量实现对LLM规划与推理的显式控制提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly being deployed to make decisions that require trading off near-term gains against long-term consequences, yet little is known about how they internally represent or resolve these tradeoffs. In this work, we causally localize an underlying subgraph for temporal preference in a distilled LLM (Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507), identifying mid-to-upper-layer nodes through converging evidence from gradient-based attribution and activation patching. We find that the geometry of time horizon is encoded in the residual stream at the expected localized layers. A behavioral analysis reveals that unintervened LLMs discount the future several times less steeply than humans, yet this preference is unstable across contexts, motivating explicit control rather than implicit reliance on training. Finally, we find suggestive evidence that steering vectors can shift temporal preference. Our work demonstrates how mechanistic interpretability can bring us closer to reliable control over how LLMs plan and reason

</details>


### [229] [State commitment learning: training language models to distinguish computation from memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05201)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Runhao Liu,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng,Huiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出状态承诺学习（State Commitment Learning）和反事实擦除强化学习（CERL），训练语言模型区分应保留的持久状态与可丢弃的临时推理痕迹，通过擦除隐藏思维后仍保持正确答案来减少模型对推理中间步骤的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前推理语言模型将所有生成的token都保留在上下文中，包括失败的尝试、死胡同和私人草稿等临时计算痕迹，这些不可靠的信息会影响后续推理。需要一种方法让模型学会区分哪些信息应作为持久状态提交，哪些可以安全丢弃。

Method: 首先定义持久状态充分性（persistent-state sufficiency）的反事实准则，使其可训练和可度量。提出反事实擦除强化学习（CERL）：在同一前缀下评估保留隐藏思维的路径和擦除隐藏思维的路径，仅在擦除路径仍然正确时给予奖励。同时引入擦除依赖性协议（Erasure Dependence Protocol）用于评估。

Result: 在数学推理、长链逻辑、科学问答和多轮工具使用等评估中，CERL显著降低了答案对隐藏思维的依赖，同时不牺牲准确率，一致优于仅正确性强化学习和长答案SFT基线。

Conclusion: 通过CERL训练，模型能够学会显式区分计算痕迹与持久状态，实现更干净、可靠的推理过程，为构建更可信的推理语言模型提供了新范式。

Abstract: Reasoning language models do not distinguish tokens used for computation from tokens that constitute persistent state: once generated, all hidden thoughts remain in context and influence future predictions. As a result, downstream reasoning may depend on failed attempts, dead ends, and private scratch work that should not be safely relied on later. We recast this phenomenon as a new training objective, state commitment learning: training models to explicitly distinguish information that should be committed as persistent state from temporary computation that can be discarded. We define a counterfactual criterion, persistent-state sufficiency, which makes it trainable and measurable whether an answer remains usable after hidden thoughts are erased. We then propose Counterfactual Erasure RL (CERL), which evaluates, under the same prefix, both a path that keeps hidden thoughts and a path that erases them, and gives reward only when the erasure path remains correct. We also introduce the Erasure Dependence Protocol and show across mathematics, long-chain logic, scientific QA, and multi-turn tool-use evaluation that CERL substantially reduces answer dependence on hidden thoughts without sacrificing accuracy, consistently outperforming correctness-only RL and long-answer SFT baselines.

</details>


### [230] [Gradient Descent with Large Step Size Restores Symmetry in Deep Linear Networks with Multi-Pathway](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05219)
*Hee-Sung Kim,Sungyoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了深度线性网络在多路径训练中，大步长梯度下降（GD）与梯度流（GF）的预测截然不同：GF预测“赢家通吃”的单路径专业化，而大步长GD在稳定性边缘的振荡会驱动网络进入再平衡阶段，使信号跨路径重新分配，最终倾向于共享表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究基于梯度流（GF）分析认为，多路径深度线性网络会通过“赢家通吃”的对称性破缺，使每个特征集中在单一通路上。本文质疑这一结论在离散梯度下降（尤其是大步长）下的适用性，试图揭示优化算法离散动力学对路径竞争机制的根本性影响。

Method: 作者对比分析了梯度流（GF）与离散梯度下降（GD）下多路径深度线性网络的动力学行为。理论上，证明了单路径解是锐利极小值（sharp minima），而跨路径分布信号可将锐度降低一个因子，该因子随路径数和网络深度增加而减小。进一步分析稳定性边缘（Edge of Stability）处的振荡行为，揭示其如何覆盖GF的对称性破缺趋势。

Result: （1）单路径解是锐利极小值，信号分散可显著降低锐度；（2）训练早期确实重现了GF预测的深度驱动对称性破缺；（3）但随后稳定性边缘处的振荡会覆盖该趋势，驱动网络进入再平衡阶段，信号重新跨路径分布；（4）最终，大步长GD倾向于共享表征而非持久的单路径主导。

Conclusion: 深度塑造了路径竞争的方式，但大步长GD的离散动力学从根本上改变了最终结果：与其收敛到单路径“赢家通吃”，网络更倾向于形成跨多条路径的共享表征。这为理解深度网络训练中优化算法选择对表征结构的影响提供了新视角。

Abstract: Recent analyses of multi-pathway Deep Linear Networks use Gradient Flow to predict a "winner-takes-all" specialization in which path symmetry breaks and each feature concentrates in a single pathway. In this work, we show that discrete Gradient Descent (GD) with a large step size tells a different story. We prove that single-path solutions are sharp minima, whereas distributing signals across pathways reduces sharpness by a factor that decreases with both the number of pathways and depth. Consequently, while early training reproduces the depth-driven symmetry breaking predicted by GF, oscillations at the Edge of Stability subsequently override this tendency and drive the network into a re-balancing phase, where signals redistribute across pathways. Together, these results clarify how depth shapes pathway competition and explain why large-step GD favors shared representations rather than persistent single-pathway dominance.

</details>


### [231] [Mitigating the Curse of Dimensionality in Uniform Convergence of Deep Neural Networks via Smooth Activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05599)
*Yizhe Ding,Runze Li,Jia Liu,Lingzhou Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为平滑激活深度神经网络（DNN）估计量建立了一致收敛的理论框架，证明ReLU网络在均匀收敛中存在维度灾难，而平滑DNN通过自适应利用目标函数的低维层次结构可缓解该问题，并在多种回归场景中获得非渐近一致收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准ReLU网络在$L^2(P)$范数下虽能达到极小极大最优速率，但其最小二乘估计量在均匀收敛中面临维度灾难。下游任务（如需要最坏情况可靠性保证的场景）对均匀收敛有实际需求，驱动作者探索平滑激活DNN作为替代方案。

Method: 作者建立了平滑DNN（包括前馈和残差结构）的伪维度界、非渐近逼近保证以及逼近算子的Hölder范数界。基于这些结果，在Huber回归、最小二乘回归、分位数回归和Logistic回归等多种统计背景下，推导出平滑DNN估计量的非渐近均匀收敛速率。

Result: 从理论上证明平滑DNN能够通过自适应利用目标函数的低维层次组合结构，缓解均匀收敛中的维度灾难。仿真研究和实际应用进一步验证了平滑DNN作为ReLU网络替代方案的可行性与理论优势。

Conclusion: 平滑DNN为需要均匀保证的统计学习任务提供了理论基础扎实且实际可行的替代方案，相较于ReLU网络在均匀收敛行为上具有显著优势。

Abstract: This paper establishes a theoretical framework for the uniform convergence of smoothly activated deep neural network (DNN) estimators. While standard ReLU networks achieve minimax-optimal rates in the $L^2(P)$ norm for various nonparametric regression tasks, we establish a theoretical lower bound demonstrating that least-squares ReLU estimators can suffer from the curse of dimensionality in their uniform convergence behavior. Motivated by the need for reliable uniform guarantees in downstream tasks requiring worst-case reliability, we address this limitation by analyzing smoothly activated DNNs (smooth DNNs), encompassing both feedforward and residual structures. We establish novel pseudo-dimension bounds, non-asymptotic approximation guarantees, and Hölder-norm bounds for the approximators of these models. Leveraging these results, we derive non-asymptotic uniform convergence rates for smooth DNN estimators across multiple statistical contexts, including Huber, least-squares, quantile, and logistic regression. We prove that smooth DNNs can mitigate the {curse of dimensionality} in uniform convergence by adaptively exploiting the low-dimensional hierarchical composition structure of the target function. Supported by both simulation studies and a real-world application, our results position smooth DNNs as a theoretically grounded and practically viable alternative to ReLU networks for statistical learning tasks requiring uniform guarantees.

</details>


### [232] [Differentiable Efficient Operator Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05232)
*Xiaohuan Pei,Jiyuan Zhang,Yuanfan Guo,Weiguo Feng,Tao Huang,Cho-Jui Hsieh,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可微分的算子搜索框架（Efficient Operator Search），将多模态基础模型中的多种手工Token缩减算子（剪枝、合并、池化、自适应重加权）统一为同一算子空间的不同状态，并通过联合搜索实现超越人工设计的混合算子，在激进视觉Token缩减下取得有竞争力的精度-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态基础模型依赖手工设计的Token缩减算子（剪枝、合并、池化、自适应重加权），这些算子看似不同但本质相关。手工设计无法穷举所有可能的算子组合，且缺乏对“在哪里缩减、保留多少Token、如何处理缩减后的信息”的联合优化，因此需要一种自动化的搜索范式来发现更优的混合算子。

Method: 提出Efficient Operator Search框架：将Token缩减算子统一参数化为一个共享的算子空间，涵盖层激活、保留预算和算子行为三个维度；构建可微分搜索策略，在单边预算和成本约束下优化任务性能；通过梯度优化联合搜索最佳的Token缩减位置、保留数量和算子类型组合。

Result: 在多个多模态基准上的实验表明，搜索得到的算子——尤其是在激进视觉Token缩减场景下——能够达到与手工设计方法竞争甚至更优的精度-效率权衡，且发现了超越孤立手工设计的混合算子。

Conclusion: 高效多模态推理可以从手工算子设计范式转向可微分算子搜索范式。所提出的搜索框架能够自动发现更优的Token缩减策略，证明了统一搜索空间和联合优化的有效性。

Abstract: Efficient multimodal foundation models often rely on manually designed token-reduction operators, such as pruning, merging, pooling, and adaptive reweighting. Although these operators appear different, we show that they can be interpreted as distinct regimes of a shared operator space. Based on this view, we introduce Efficient Operator Search, a differentiable framework that jointly searches where to reduce tokens, how many tokens to retain, and how reduced token information should be processed. The proposed search space parameterizes layer activation, retention budget, and operator behavior, while the search policy optimizes task performance under one-sided budget and cost constraints. This formulation recovers representative hand-designed baselines as special cases and further discovers hybrid operators beyond isolated manual designs. Experiments on multimodal benchmarks show that the searched operators achieve competitive accuracy-efficiency trade-offs, especially under aggressive visual-token reduction. These results suggest that efficient multimodal inference can be reframed from manual operator design to differentiable operator search.

</details>


### [233] [DiffSlack: Learning under Nonlinear Inequality Constraints via Learnable Slack Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05247)
*Ziqian Wang,Chenxi Fang,Zhen Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DiffSlack 提出一种可微投影层，通过将非线性不等式约束转化为带可学习松弛变量的等式约束，并结合阻尼高斯-牛顿投影，实现神经网络输出对大规模耦合非线性不等式约束的硬约束嵌入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络硬约束方法在处理大规模非线性不等式约束时，要么对约束集施加结构性限制，要么引入显著的计算开销。尤其是在车辆路径规划等工程应用中，输出需同时满足多达200个耦合的非线性不等式约束（碰撞避免、曲率限制、航点间距等），急需一种可扩展、可微且高效的约束满足方案。

Method: 提出 DiffSlack 框架：(1) 将不等式约束重构为带可学习松弛变量的等式约束，松弛变量由增强网络输出预测，为后续投影提供数据驱动的热启动；(2) 设计阻尼高斯-牛顿投影层，将原始预测映射到增广可行流形上，保持端到端可微性；(3) 引入两阶段课程训练策略以稳定训练并提升约束满足率。

Result: 在包含200个非线性不等式约束的车辆路径规划任务上，DiffSlack 相比现有基于学习的方法获得了更高的规划成功率和更强的几何约束满足度，推理开销相当。消融实验表明硬投影层降低了对监督质量的敏感性。CARLA 闭环跟踪和实车实验验证了生成轨迹的可执行性。

Conclusion: DiffSlack 为将硬不等式约束嵌入神经网络提供了一种实用且可扩展的方法，在工程应用中具有良好前景。

Abstract: Enforcing nonlinear inequality constraints in neural networks remains challenging, especially when the output is subject to many coupled constraints. Existing hard constraint methods often impose structural restrictions on the constraint set or introduce substantial computational overhead for large-scale nonlinear problems. Here, we propose DiffSlack, a differentiable projection layer for nonlinear inequality-constrained neural prediction. DiffSlack reformulates inequalities as equalities with learnable slack variables, which are predicted as part of the augmented network output and provide a data-driven warm start for damped Gauss-Newton projection. The projection layer maps raw predictions onto the augmented feasible manifold while preserving end-to-end differentiability. A two-stage curriculum further stabilizes training and improves constraint satisfaction. We evaluate DiffSlack on vehicle path planning with 200 nonlinear inequality constraints from collision avoidance, curvature limits, and waypoint spacing. Compared with existing learning-based baselines, DiffSlack achieves a higher planning success rate and stronger geometric constraint satisfaction under a comparable inference budget. Ablation studies further show that the hard projection layer reduces sensitivity to supervision quality. Closed-loop tracking in CARLA and real-world vehicle experiments confirms the executability of the generated trajectories. These results demonstrate that DiffSlack provides a practical and scalable approach to embedding hard inequality constraints into neural networks for engineering applications.

</details>


### [234] [Alpha-RTL: Test-Time Training for RTL Hardware Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05253)
*Peilong Zhou,Zhirong Chen,Cangyuan Li,Haoyu Gao,Kaiyan Chang,Ziming Qu,Ying Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TTT-RTL 是首个针对RTL设计的测试时训练框架，通过在测试阶段利用EDA工具的可执行反馈（语法检查、仿真、综合PPA评分）对LLM策略进行强化学习微调，结合PUCT设计状态池和自适应KL预算控制器，在RTLLM v2.0和工业级C910 FPU上分别实现PPA乘积降低65.1%和ADP降低59.4%，显著优于冻结策略基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成RTL的方法分为两类：一是在部署前用EDA反馈训练通用RTL生成器，二是在测试时用冻结策略进行搜索。前者缺乏对特定设计问题的针对性优化，后者无法根据实时反馈适应策略。本文旨在填补这一空白，实现测试时根据特定RTL问题的可执行EDA反馈进行策略自适应优化。

Method: TTT-RTL框架包含四个核心组件：(1) 从LLM策略采样候选RTL实现；(2) 通过语法检查和仿真进行验证，利用综合得到的PPA乘积对有效设计评分；(3) 基于PUCT索引的设计状态池重用高奖励变体；(4) 使用熵正则策略梯度目标更新策略。此外引入自适应KL预算控制器，综合参考KL散度、有效样本量和奖励饱和信号来调整熵约束，以稳定稀疏或平台化奖励下的策略更新。

Result: 在Nangate 45nm工艺下RTLLM v2.0基准上，TTT-RTL将几何平均PPA乘积降低65.1%，优于最强冻结策略基线的26.1%。在Sky130工艺下工业级玄铁C910 FPU前导零预测单元上，TTT-RTL实现59.4%的ADP降低。消融实验验证了策略自适应、状态重用和KL预算控制各自均有贡献。

Conclusion: 测试时训练结合可执行EDA反馈，能够推动基于LLM的RTL生成超越功能正确性，迈向物理优化的硬件设计。TTT-RTL首次证明了针对具体设计问题进行在线策略自适应的可行性和显著效果。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown increasing promise in generating
  functionally correct register-transfer-level (RTL) hardware designs.
  Recent systems improve further through EDA-integrated reinforcement
  learning with syntax, simulation, and PPA rewards, but train a general
  RTL generator before deployment while test-time approaches search with
  a frozen policy. We instead perform reinforcement learning at test time,
  allowing the LLM policy to adapt to executable EDA feedback for the
  specific RTL problem at hand. We propose TTT-RTL, to our knowledge the
  first per-design test-time training framework that closes the loop
  between an LLM policy and an EDA pipeline for RTL optimization. TTT-RTL
  samples candidate implementations, verifies them through syntax checking
  and simulation, scores valid designs using synthesis-derived PPA product,
  reuses high-reward variants through a PUCT-indexed design-state pool,
  and updates the policy with an entropic policy-gradient objective. To
  stabilize policy updates under sparse or plateaued rewards, we introduce
  an adaptive KL-budget controller that adjusts the entropy constraint
  using reference KL, effective sample size, and reward saturation signals.
  On RTLLM v2.0 under Nangate 45nm, TTT-RTL reduces the geometric-mean
  PPA product by 65.1% over the reference, outperforming the strongest
  published frozen-policy agent baseline at 26.1%. On an industrial
  XuanTie C910 FPU leading-zero-anticipation unit under Sky130, TTT-RTL
  achieves a 59.4% ADP reduction, and ablations confirm that policy
  adaptation, state reuse, and KL-budget control each contribute. These
  results suggest that test-time training with executable EDA feedback can
  move LLM-based RTL generation beyond functional correctness toward
  physically optimized hardware.

</details>


### [235] [Flash-WAM: Modality-Aware Distillation for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05254)
*Arman Akbari,Ci Zhang,Arash Akbari,Lin Zhao,Yixiao Chen,Weiwei Chen,Xuan Zhang,Geng Yuan,Yanzhi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Flash-WAM提出一种模态感知的步数蒸馏框架，针对视频-动作联合扩散模型，分别为动作流（低噪声）和视频流（高噪声）设计不同的一致性函数参数化方案，将推理压缩至单步，实现23倍加速，同时保持操作任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: World-action models (WAMs) 虽在操作基准上表现强劲，但其迭代扩散过程需数十步去噪，无法满足实时控制需求。现有步数蒸馏方法直接应用于视频-动作联合生成时会失效，因为视频和动作流使用不同的SNR偏移噪声调度（video使用高噪声保持方差，action使用低噪声接近干净数据），导致训练时两者的边际噪声分布显著不对称，单模态蒸馏方法无法处理这种差异。

Method: 提出Flash-WAM，一种模态感知的一致性蒸馏框架：(1) 基于一致性函数族的理论分析，刻画在一致性边界条件下的梯度缩放特性；(2) 对动作流的低噪声区域采用线性梯度缩放参数化（linear-gradient-scaling parametrization）；(3) 对视频流的高噪声区域采用方差保持参数化（variance-preserving parametrization）；(4) 在LingBot-VA模型上实例化，将各模态推理压缩至单步。

Result: 在NVIDIA L40S上，单chunk推理延迟从8.1秒降至348毫秒（23倍加速），实现实时推理。RoboTwin 2.0仿真基准任务成功率达85.5%，LIBERO达95.7%；在Unitree G1人形机器人真实世界中平均恢复60%的性能。相比之下，naive一致性蒸馏在相同步数预算下成功率仅24%。

Conclusion: Flash-WAM通过模态感知的一致性蒸馏设计，有效解决了视频-动作联合扩散模型中不对称噪声分布带来的蒸馏难题，首次将WAM推理压缩至单步实时运行，在仿真和真实机器人平台上验证了其有效性，为实时机器人操控提供了可行方案。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) jointly generate future video and robot actions through iterative diffusion, achieving strong performance on manipulation benchmarks but requiring tens of denoising steps, a cost that precludes real-time control. Step distillation has emerged as the natural remedy, but off-the-shelf methods break down in the joint video-action setting because video and action streams use different SNR-shifted noise schedules and reach training with substantially different marginal noise distributions, an asymmetry that single-modality distillation methods cannot accommodate. We introduce \textbf{Flash-WAM}, a modality-aware step-distillation framework inspired by consistency distillation that selects the consistency function for each modality to match its noise regime: a linear-gradient-scaling parametrization for the action stream's low-noise regime, paired with a variance-preserving parametrization for the video stream's high-noise regime, grounded in a structural analysis of the consistency-function family that characterizes the achievable gradient scaling under the consistency boundary condition. Instantiated on LingBot-VA, Flash-WAM compresses inference to a single step in each modality. On RoboTwin 2.0, this reduces per-chunk latency from $8.1$ seconds to $348$ ms on NVIDIA L40S, a $23{\times}$ speedup that enables real-time inference. Flash-WAM preserves task success on simulation benchmarks ($85.5\%$ RoboTwin 2.0, $95.7\%$ LIBERO) and substantially recovers real-world performance ($60\%$ average on a Unitree G1 humanoid robot), while naive consistency distillation drops to $24\%$ at the same step budget.

</details>


### [236] [Scaling Laws for Behavioral Foundation Models over User Event Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05257)
*Rickard Brüel Gabrielsson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了行为基础模型（behavioral foundation models）的缩放定律（scaling laws），聚焦于"特征嵌入器 + 仅解码器Transformer"的两部分架构。通过约600次真实交互数据实验（10¹⁵–10¹⁹ FLOPs），作者发现：小嵌入器（约2%参数）在所有算力预算下均为计算最优；计算最优的D/N比随算力增加向Chinchilla启发式靠拢；负采样数量、关键批量大小、损失与排序指标的一致性等均随算力变化而缩放。核心结论是评估指标本身构成缩放定律的一部分，改变评估指标可能改变计算最优配方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前推荐、支付、欺诈检测等领域的序列行为模型虽被广泛部署，但缺乏类似语言模型领域中缩放定律（如Chinchilla）所提供的那种计算校准指导。实际部署中需要同时权衡嵌入器与上下文编码器的参数分配、批量大小、模型/数据配比及负采样数量等多个轴，却缺乏系统性经验指导。该研究旨在填补这一空白，为行为基础模型建立多轴缩放定律。

Method: 采用两阶段行为模型架构：基于特征的嵌入器将多模态事件映射为向量，随后仅解码器Transformer基于序列预测下一事件。在真实用户交互数据上执行约600次训练运行，算力跨度从10¹⁵到10¹⁹ FLOPs。联合变化四个部署相关维度：(1) 两部分参数分割比；(2) 关键批量大小；(3) 模型规模与数据量的分配（D/N比）；(4) 冻结嵌入器后使用的负采样数量。通过损失函数与排序质量指标的联合分析，揭示这些维度随算力的缩放行为。

Result: (1) 嵌入器参数占比约2%（s⋆≈2%）在所有算力预算下均为计算最优，因其每步计算成本更高且接触大量重复项；(2) 低算力时，计算最优训练相对文本模型更偏数据密集型，但随着算力增加，D/N比趋近Chinchilla启发式（模型与数据等比缩放）；(3) 关键批量大小、冻结后最优负采样数、损失与排序指标的一致性均随算力及所选评估指标而变化；(4) 在10¹⁹ FLOPs时，负采样的主动约束变为候选轴内存而非FLOPs；更大算力预算下，更多负样本更优。

Conclusion: 在行为基础模型中，评估指标的选择本身就是缩放定律的内在组成部分——改变评估指标可能导致计算最优训练配方的改变。此外，嵌入器与上下文编码器的参数分配存在稳定的计算最优分割点（约2%），这源于两者在计算成本和数据暴露量上的不对称性。这些发现为行为序列模型的工业部署提供了系统性的计算校准指导。

Abstract: Foundation models are increasingly trained on sequences of user actions in recommendation, payments, fraud, and commerce, but these models still lack the kind of compute calibration that scaling laws provide for language models. We study a common two-part behavioral-model architecture: a feature-based event embedder maps each multi-modal item to a vector, and a decoder-only transformer predicts the next event from the resulting sequence. Across roughly 600 runs on real interaction data, spanning $10^{15}$-$10^{19}$ training FLOPs, we jointly vary four deployment-relevant axes: the two-part parameter split, critical batch size, model/data allocation, and the number of sampled negatives used after freezing the embedder. A small embedder ($s^{\star}\!\approx\!2\%$ of parameters) is compute-optimal at every budget we test because embedder parameters are both more expensive per step and exposed to far more repeated items than contextualizer parameters. Compute-optimal training is data-heavy relative to text at low compute, but its $D/N$ ratio moves toward the Chinchilla heuristic as compute increases. The sampled training objective and deployed ranking metrics disagree in ways that themselves scale: critical batch size, optimal negative count after freezing, and the agreement between loss and ranking quality all shift with compute and with the chosen evaluation metric. For negative sampling, larger budgets increasingly prefer more negatives; by $10^{19}$ FLOPs the active constraint is candidate-axis memory rather than FLOPs. In behavioral foundation models, the evaluation metric is therefore part of the scaling law: changing it can change the compute-optimal recipe.

</details>


### [237] [Policy-Conditioned Counterfactual Credit for Verifiable Reinforcement Learning of Long-Horizon Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05263)
*Renwei Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CVT-RL 提出了一种带约束的策略梯度算法，通过干预有效性门控和策略条件反事实贡献估计器（PCCC），为语言智能体提供密集的可验证奖励，从而减少长程任务中的无支撑证据链、信念漂移和捷径行为，在多个基准上将成功率从 71.8–75.4% 提升至 78.9%，并将 hacking 率降至 3.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长程语言智能体的过程奖励多为相关性导向，仅奖励检索、反思或验证类步骤的外观，而未评估该步骤在特定干预下是否对最终可验证成功具有因果贡献，导致模型学会无支撑的证据链、信念漂移和仅满足终端检查的捷径动作。因此需要一个基于反事实因果推断的密集奖励框架来归因步骤的因果贡献。

Method: 提出 CVT-RL，核心包括：(1) 四种受控干预——删除、语义替换、证据替换和工具输出扰动，用以隔离步骤的因果效应；(2) 策略条件反事实贡献估计器（PCCC），从冻结的参考策略中采样后续，并使用选择调整的双重鲁棒估计器增强优势函数；(3) 干预有效性门控，仅允许前缀可观测标签进行信念控制；(4) 增广拉格朗日约束，限制无支撑主张、跳过验证、工具篡改和不安全调用。

Result: 在长上下文QA、ALFWorld、ScienceWorld 和 web/tool 任务上，CVT-RL 平均成功率达 78.9%，优于计算匹配的非因果 RL（71.8%）和信息匹配的反事实过程基线（75.4%）；证据 F1 从 78.9 提升至 82.8；测量 hacking 从 7.2% 降至 3.9%；独立人工审计 hacking 为 4.6%（基线 8.1%）；自适应检测器规避攻击下 hacking 仅升至 7.1%；分层 bootstrap 和混合效应检验经 Holm 校正后 p<0.01。

Conclusion: 精心限定的反事实信用分配，结合有效性门控、诊断和可验证约束，为更可靠的长程语言智能体强化学习提供了一条可复现的路径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards improves reasoning and tool use, yet long-horizon language agents still learn unsupported evidence chains, belief drift, and shortcut actions that satisfy terminal checks. Existing process rewards are mostly correlational: they reward retrieval-, reflection-, or verification-like steps without estimating whether the step contributes to final verified success under a specified intervention. We propose CVT-RL, a constrained policy-gradient algorithm with dense verifiable rewards, intervention-validity gating, and a policy-conditioned counterfactual contribution (PCCC) estimator. Deletion, semantic substitution, evidence substitution, and tool-output perturbation define separate controlled interventions; continuations are sampled from a frozen reference policy, and a selection-adjusted doubly robust estimator augments the advantage. Belief control uses only prefix-observable labels, while an augmented Lagrangian constrains unsupported claims, skipped verification, tool tampering, and unsafe calls. On long-context QA, ALFWorld, ScienceWorld, and web/tool tasks, CVT-RL improves average task success from 71.8% for compute-matched non-causal RL and 75.4% for an information-matched counterfactual-process baseline to 78.9%, improves evidence F1 from 78.9 to 82.8 over the information-matched baseline, and reduces measured hacking from 7.2% to 3.9%. Independent human audit estimates 4.6% hacking for CVT-RL versus 8.1% for the information-matched baseline, and adaptive detector-evasion attacks raise hacking only to 7.1%. Stratified bootstrap and mixed-effects tests give p<0.01 after Holm correction for all primary metrics. Carefully scoped counterfactual credit, paired with validity gating, diagnostics, and verifiable constraints, provides a reproducible route toward more reliable long-horizon RL for language agents.

</details>


### [238] [REGEN: Reference-Guided Synthetic Multivariate Time Series Generation for Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05264)
*Moulik Gupta,Dhruv Kumar,Murari Mandal,Saurabh Deshpande*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReGeN 是一种参考引导的生成框架，将观察序列分解为周期性主干、深度核高斯过程残差和结构因果模型驱动的跨变量依赖三个可解释组件，通过可控温度采样生成保留域结构的高质量合成数据，在低数据场景中表现优于现有生成器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元时间序列预测模型的训练需要大规模多样化的语料，但许多现实领域仅能提供极少量的观测序列。现有生成方法存在两难：基于先验的方法产生与域无关的样本，而数据驱动方法将参考数据视为黑箱监督，丧失了对周期结构、局部变异性和跨变量动态的显式控制。

Method: ReGeN 将每条参考序列分解为三个可解释组件：(1) 相位对齐的周期主干，捕获主导域的形态特征；(2) 使用深度核高斯过程建模的每变量随机残差；(3) 通过结构因果模型注入的滞后感知跨变量依赖，配有拟合的耦合系数。通过对这些组件以可控温度进行采样，在保留域结构的同时扩大分布覆盖范围。

Result: ReGeN 生成的数据可持续替代真实同源数据且预测性能下降极小；在交通等强周期域中甚至可超越真实数据。基于 ReGeN 语料预训练的基础模型优于基于先验方法和数据驱动方法合成数据预训练的模型。

Conclusion: 在低数据场景下，如何结构化地利用参考数据可能与拥有多少数据同等重要。ReGeN 通过将参考序列作为结构支架而非模仿样本，实现了可控、可解释且域一致的合成数据生成。

Abstract: Training robust multivariate time series forecasting models requires large, diverse corpora, yet many real-world domains provide only a handful of observed sequences. Existing generators fail to resolve this mismatch: prior-based approaches (e.g., CauKer, TimePFN) produce domain-agnostic samples, while data-driven methods (e.g., TimeGAN) treat references as black-box supervision, forfeiting explicit control over periodic structure, local variability, and cross-variable dynamics.
  We propose ReGeN, a reference-guided generative pipeline that treats observed sequences not as examples to imitate, but as structural scaffolds for controllable synthesis. ReGeN decomposes each reference into three interpretable components: a phase-aligned periodic backbone capturing dominant domain morphology; per-variable stochastic residuals modeled with a deep-kernel Gaussian process; and lag-aware cross-variable dependencies injected through a structural causal model with fitted coupling coefficients. Sampling these components at controllable temperature broadens distributional coverage while preserving domain-grounded structure.
  We show that ReGeN-generated data consistently substitutes for real sibling data with minimal forecasting degradation, and in strongly periodic domains such as traffic, can outperform the real source itself. We further show that a foundation model pretrained on ReGeN corpora outperforms those pretrained on prior-based and data-driven synthetic alternatives. This suggests that in low-data regimes, how reference data is structurally exploited can matter as much as how much data is available.

</details>


### [239] [Data-efficient flood depth prediction through domain-aware coreset selection and tabular foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05265)
*Lipai Huang,Adithi Srinath,Manas Singh,Junwei Ma,Ali Mostafavi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种领域感知的核心集构建方法，在推理时对表格基础模型进行条件化，仅用每流域0.7%的训练数据即可实现跨流域可迁移的洪水深度预测，准确度达到监督参考模型的98.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近实时洪水深度预测需要准确、快速且可跨流域迁移的替代模型。现有监督替代模型虽精度高，但每个流域需数百万训练行且无法外推至原始网格之外，数据效率低、迁移性差。

Method: 提出领域感知的核心集构建流程：(1) 按重现期和最受影响流域对暴雨事件进行分层；(2) 使用目标感知的空间选择器采样六边形网格单元；(3) 在推理时用构建的核心集条件化表格基础模型，实现少样本预测。仅使用每流域训练池的0.7%数据。

Result: 在休斯顿地区9个流域上，模型取得平均R²=0.663，达到监督参考模型(R²=0.673)的98.5%。可迁移至未参与训练的流域而无须任务特定重训练，优于核心集训练的监督基线。在真实暴雨事件中，远分布外案例表现超越监督参考模型，近分布内案例略逊。

Conclusion: 领域感知的核心集构建使表格基础模型能够实现数据高效、流域可迁移的洪水预测，无需逐流域训练，为近实时洪水深度预测提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Near-real-time flood depth prediction demands surrogate models that are accurate, fast, and transferable across watersheds. Supervised surrogates can match physics-based simulators in accuracy but need millions of training rows per watershed and cannot extrapolate beyond their original mesh. We propose a domain-aware coreset construction pipeline that conditions a tabular foundation model at inference time. The pipeline stratifies storms by return period and most-affected watershed, then samples hexagons with a target-aware spatial selector. With 0.7% of the per-watershed training pool, the model attains a mean $R^2$ of 0.663 across nine Houston-area watersheds, within 98.5% of the supervised reference ($R^2$ = 0.673). It transfers to held-out watersheds without task-specific retraining, staying ahead of a coreset-trained supervised baseline. On real storms it exceeds the supervised reference on a far out-of-distribution case and trails it on a mostly in-distribution one. Domain-aware coreset construction lets tabular foundation models deliver data-efficient, watershed-transferable flood predictions without per-watershed training.

</details>


### [240] [Sharp Low-Degree Thresholds for Planted-vs-Planted Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05266)
*Anda Skeja,Daniel Gutiérrez Espinoza,Fiona Skerman,Alexander S. Wein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在"种植-对-种植"(planted-vs-planted)设定下，首次建立了低次多项式检验的尖锐阈值，证明了在种植子矩阵和种植稠密子图模型中，社区计数检验的上下界匹配，且该检验阈值与已知的低次恢复阈值在尖锐常数级别上一致；同时识别出弱检验存在平滑过渡而非尖锐阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低次多项式检验的研究主要集中于"种植-对-零"(planted-vs-null)设定，即区分结构化种植模型与纯噪声。然而，许多实际场景需要区分两种不同的结构化种植机制（planted-vs-planted），该设定下的低次检验尖锐阈值尚属空白。作者旨在填补这一理论缺口，揭示种植-对-种植设定下检验阈值与恢复阈值之间的内在联系。

Method: 1) 构建基于潜变量展开(latent-variable expansion)的统一框架，将低次恢复中的展开技术推广至种植-对-种植检验；2) 开发识别并剪除"非信号贡献"(non-signal contributions)的新方法，以精确刻画低次多项式的判别能力；3) 在种植子矩阵模型和种植稠密子图模型中，针对社区计数问题分别建立匹配的低次上界和下界；4) 对弱检验(weak testing)任务进行精细分析，揭示其平滑过渡行为。

Result: 1) 在种植子矩阵和种植稠密子图模型中，社区计数的低次检验阈值与低次恢复阈值在尖锐常数级别上完全一致；2) 弱检验（仅需优于随机猜测）不存在尖锐阈值，而是呈现平滑过渡，作者精确刻画了该过渡行为。

Conclusion: 种植-对-种植设定下的低次多项式检验首次被证明具有尖锐阈值，且该阈值与恢复阈值重合，揭示了检验与恢复之间的深刻对偶关系。弱检验的平滑过渡现象则表明，不同强度的检验目标会导致本质上不同的阈值行为。所发展的潜变量展开与信号剪除框架为后续相关研究提供了有力的技术工具。

Abstract: We establish the first sharp thresholds for low-degree polynomial tests in planted-vs-planted settings, where the goal is to determine with vanishing error which of two structured planted mechanisms generated the observed data. We prove matching low-degree upper and lower bounds for counting communities in the planted submatrix and planted dense subgraph models. The resulting testing threshold coincides, down to the sharp constant, with the known low-degree recovery threshold. In contrast, the task of weak testing, where the goal is to outperform random guessing, does not have a sharp threshold but rather a smooth transition, which we identify. To prove our results, we develop a framework for planted-vs-planted testing that builds on a latent-variable expansion originating in low-degree recovery and employs new methods to identify and prune non-signal contributions.

</details>


### [241] [Learning Manifold and Itô Dynamics with Branched Neural Rough Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05272)
*Luke Thompson,Dai Shi,Lequan Lin,Junbin Gao,Andi Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分支神经粗糙微分方程（B-NRDEs），通过Hopf代数框架将NRDE的log-ODE步重新解释为状态空间流形上的几何数值积分，使驱动代数与微积分类型匹配：Euclidean Itô动力学用Grossman-Larson有根树、流形上有序协变导数用Munthe-Kaas-Wright平面有根树、经典Stratonovich情形用shuffle代数。此框架可精确保持流形约束，并引入分支签名核目标函数使训练中能捕捉二次变差项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经粗糙微分方程（NRDEs）依赖shuffle代数，无法暴露Itô动力学所需的二次变差项，也无法处理配备联络的流形上有序协变导数。因此需要一种统一框架，将NRDE扩展到Euclidean-Stratonovich设定之外，支持Itô微积分和流形值动力学。

Method: 提出B-NRDEs框架，将NRDE的log-ODE步重新定义为状态空间流形上的几何数值积分。核心是根据不同微积分类型匹配驱动代数：Itô情形使用Grossman-Larson有根树对应的Hopf代数，流形上有序协变导数使用Munthe-Kaas-Wright平面有根树，Stratonovich情形沿用shuffle代数。此外引入分支签名核目标函数，使训练时二次变差项可见，实现Itô一致的分布匹配。

Result: 在粗糙Bergomi波动率建模、sim-to-real SO(3)动力学预测、SPD协方差动力学三个任务上验证了B-NRDEs的有效性，展示了统一的、超出Euclidean-Stratonovich设定的随机流形值动力学建模能力。

Conclusion: B-NRDEs为随机微分方程和流形值动力学提供了统一的几何数值积分框架，通过匹配驱动代数与目标微积分类型，实现了对Itô二次变差项和流形上协变导数的精确建模，在多样化的实际任务中表现出有效性。

Abstract: Neural rough differential equations (NRDEs) stay accurate under irregular sampling while taking far fewer integration steps than standard neural differential equations, summarising a finely sampled driver by its log-signature and advancing the hidden state over coarse intervals using the log-ODE method. This efficiency rests on the shuffle algebra, the algebraic counterpart of Stratonovich calculus. This reliance means NRDEs cannot expose the quadratic-variation terms Itô dynamics require, nor the ordered covariant derivatives that govern Itô flows on connection-equipped manifolds. Ameliorating this, we introduce Branched Neural Rough Differential Equations (B-NRDEs), a Hopf-algebraic framework that recasts the NRDE log-ODE step as geometric numerical integration on the state-space manifold, matching the driving algebra to the governing calculus: Grossman--Larson rooted trees for Euclidean Itô dynamics, Munthe-Kaas--Wright planar rooted trees for ordered covariant derivatives on manifolds, and the shuffle algebra in the classical Stratonovich case. This yields intrinsic coarse-step dynamics that exactly preserve manifold constraints. Finally, we introduce a branched signature-kernel objective to enable Itô-consistent law matching by making quadratic-variation terms visible during training. On rough Bergomi volatility, sim-to-real $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ dynamics forecasting, and SPD covariance dynamics, B-NRDEs offer a unified, effective approach to stochastic and manifold-valued dynamics beyond the Euclidean--Stratonovich setting.

</details>


### [242] [Anomaly Detection for Electro-Hydrostatic Actuators using LSTM Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05274)
*Nehal Afifi,Abdelmonem Elhendawi,Felix Leitenberger,Nadine Piat,Sven Matthiesen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于LSTM自编码器的离线异常检测框架，用于EHA传感器单变量信号（温度/压力），在多种故障注入场景下平均准确率达99.0%，F1分数最高99.8%，显著优于传统统计和经典机器学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EHA传感器数据量大、采样频率高，传统方法（Z-score、IQR、孤立森林等）无法捕捉信号的时间依赖性，导致检测精度有限、误报率高；且缺乏对不同工况下数据驱动异常检测方法的系统评估。

Method: 采用基于重构的LSTM自编码器架构，利用验证集重构误差分布进行校准与阈值设定。在受控测试台采集温度与压力传感器数据，通过多场景故障注入评估性能，并进行不同工况下的灵敏度分析。评估指标包括准确率、精确率、召回率和F1分数。

Result: LSTM自编码器平均准确率达99.0%，精确率最高100%，召回率90.2%-99.6%，F1分数93.1%-99.8%，在所有评估传感器上均表现出高检测灵敏度和极低误报率，全面优于对比的传统方法。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的离线异常检测方法对EHA系统是可行的。未来工作将聚焦于将该框架适配至在线（实时）环境。

Abstract: Electro-Hydrostatic Actuators (EHAs) are widely used in aerospace and industrial systems, where timely detection of sensor anomalies is essential to ensure safe and reliable operation. However, the large volume and high sampling frequency of EHA sensor data pose challenges for accurate and efficient anomaly detection. Conventional statistical and classical machine-learning methods such as Z-score, Interquartile Range (IQR), Median Absolute Deviation (MAD), Isolation Forest, Gaussian Mixture, and k-means often fail to capture the temporal dependencies inherent in EHA signals, resulting in limited detection accuracy and elevated false-alarm rates. Furthermore, systematic evaluations of data-driven anomaly detection approaches for EHA systems remain scarce, particularly under varying operational conditions. This study presents an offline anomaly-detection framework for univariate EHA sensor signals, focusing on temperature and pressure data collected from a controlled test bench. The method employs a reconstruction-based Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) autoencoder, calibrated and evaluated using validation-set reconstruction-error distributions. Performance is assessed across multiple fault-injection scenarios using accuracy, precision, recall, and F1-score, complemented by sensitivity analyses under varying operating conditions. The LSTM autoencoder achieved an average accuracy of 99.0\%, precision up to 100\%, recall between 90.2\% and 99.6\%, and F1-scores from 93.1\% to 99.8\%, demonstrating high detection sensitivity and a very low false-alarm rate across all evaluated sensors. These results highlight the feasibility of data-driven offline anomaly detection for EHAs. Future work will focus on adapting the developed framework for an online (real-time) environment.

</details>


### [243] [Agentic Monte Carlo: Simulating Reinforcement Learning for Black-Box Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05296)
*Dae Yon Hwang,Raunaq Suri,Valentin Villecroze,Anthony L. Caterini,Jesse C. Cresswell,Noël Vouitsis,Brendan Leigh Ross*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Monte Carlo (AMC)，利用RL与贝叶斯推断的等价性，通过序贯蒙特卡洛方法直接从黑盒LLM智能体的最优策略中采样，无需参数级优化即可实现类RL优化，在AgentGym上超越提示基线并随测试时计算扩展超越GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体分为可进行强化学习的开源权重智能体和仅能在测试时控制的黑盒智能体。黑盒智能体虽有最先进的私有LLM支持，但仅API访问使参数级优化不可行，大多数RL方法无法应用。

Method: 基于RL与贝叶斯推断的等价性，将最优策略建模为轨迹上的后验分布，以固定黑盒LLM智能体为先验。采用序贯蒙特卡洛(SMC)方法，通过学习价值函数引导智能体采样，从该后验中直接采样，而不修改底层黑盒模型。

Result: 在AgentGym基准的三个不同环境中验证，AMC显著优于提示基线方法，且随着测试时计算量扩展，性能超越Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)。

Conclusion: AMC证明了在不访问模型参数的情况下对黑盒LLM智能体进行原则性RL风格优化的可行性，为黑盒智能体的优化开辟了新路径。

Abstract: LLM agents operate in two distinct regimes: open-weight agents amenable to reinforcement learning (RL) and black-box agents whose behaviour must be controlled purely at test time. Although black-box agents are often backed by state-of-the-art proprietary LLMs, API-only access precludes parameter-level optimization, rendering most RL methods inapplicable. To address this limitation, we turn to a known equivalence between RL and Bayesian inference. We propose Agentic Monte Carlo (AMC) to directly sample from the optimal policy of a black-box agent rather than training it through RL. The optimal policy is a posterior over trajectories whose prior we define as the fixed black-box LLM agent. We employ Sequential Monte Carlo to sample from this posterior by learning a value function to steer the agent while leaving the underlying black-box model unchanged. We validate AMC on three diverse environments from the AgentGym benchmark, demonstrating significant improvements over prompting baselines and even outperforming Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) as we scale the test-time compute of our method. AMC demonstrates the feasibility of performing principled RL-style optimization of black-box LLM agents. Code is available at https://github.com/layer6ai-labs/Agentic-Monte-Carlo

</details>


### [244] [Statistically Reliable LLM-Based Ranking Evaluation via Prediction-Powered Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05308)
*Abhishek Divekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PRECISE框架，将预测驱动推断（PPI）扩展到排序评估指标，通过结合少量人工标注与大量LLM评判数据，生成偏差校正的指标估计。该方法在理论上对LLM评判器的误差分布具有无偏性，并通过将输出空间计算复杂度从O(2^|C|)降至O(2^K)，使其适用于Precision@K等层级指标。实验表明，仅需30个人工标注即可将标准误差降低21%，并在生产系统中成功识别最优系统变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 排序评估指标（如Precision@K）的可靠估计通常依赖大量昂贵的人工标注。LLM评判器成本低但存在系统性偏差，直接使用会导致错误结论。现有PPI方法无法直接处理排序指标中"标注粒度在文档级、指标粒度在查询级"的层级结构，需要一种既能利用LLM效率又能保证统计有效性的新方法。

Method: 将预测驱动推断（PPI）框架扩展到排序评估场景。核心创新在于处理层级指标时的计算优化：通过归约策略将输出空间的计算复杂度从O(2^|C|)降至O(2^K)，其中|C|为候选文档集大小，K为截断值。方法利用少量人工标注构建偏差校正项，结合大规模LLM评判数据，生成渐进无偏且方差更低的指标估计量。

Result: 在ESCI基准上，使用Claude 3 Sonnet评判并仅增加30个人工标注，将Precision@4估计的标准误差从4.45降至3.50（相对降低21%）。在生产系统中，仅用100个人工标签和2小时领域专家标注，即正确识别出三个系统变体中的最优者；后续A/B测试验证了该排名，最优系统带来每日销售额+407个基点的提升。

Conclusion: PRECISE成功将PPI扩展到层级排序指标，在保持统计无偏性的同时显著降低了对人工标注的依赖。该方法兼具理论严谨性与实际可部署性，在学术基准和生产环境中均得到验证，为LLM辅助的排序系统评估提供了可靠框架。
