Method: 构建三个专门数据集：(1) 跨越长历史时期的视觉相似对象数据集；(2) 按多样化事件和对象类型分类的数据集；(3) 图像与时间敏感新闻文本配对的数据集，用于跨模态对齐评估。通过在这些数据集上进行大规模实验，分析模型在不同类别上的性能差异，并特别检验模型是否依赖图像颜色等表面特征作为"捷径"。

Result: 实验表明，VLM在时序推理任务中表现出一定能力，但存在显著的类别间性能差异。关键发现是：模型频繁利用灰度与彩色滤镜等表面线索来绕过真正的时序推理，而非基于实质性的年代特征做出判断。

Conclusion: ChronoVision为诊断VLM时序推理局限性提供了高质量数据集和严格评估框架，揭示了当前模型依赖表面捷径的核心问题。该工作为未来开发更稳健、逻辑更扎实的多模态模型指明了方向。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have significantly enhanced their ability to interpret complex visual semantics, yet their capacity for chronological reasoning remains under-explored. In this paper, we introduce a novel benchmark specifically designed to evaluate how VLMs perceive and reason about chronological information within and across images. Unlike existing video-based benchmarks that focus on frame sequencing, our work delves into the underlying logic of chronological judgment and the expansion toward multimodal integration. To facilitate this, we construct three specialized datasets: one containing visually similar objects spanning long historical durations, another categorized by diverse event and object types, and a third pairing images with time-sensitive news text for cross-modal alignment. Through extensive experiments, we analyze whether models exhibit performance disparities across categories and, crucially, explore whether they rely on ``incorrect shortcuts'', such as image color rather than genuine chronological features. Our results reveal that while VLMs show promise, they frequently exploit superficial cues like grayscale versus color filters to bypass authentic chronological reasoning. By providing these high-quality datasets and a rigorous evaluation framework, we offer a diagnostic tool to identify current limitations and guide the development of more robust, logically grounded multimodal models. The source code is shown in https://github.com/LuoRenqiang/ChronoVision.

</details>


### [352] [Entropy-Based Evaluation of AI Agents: A Lightweight Framework for Measuring Behavioral Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05872)
*Olasimbo Ayodeji Arigbabu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于熵的AI Agent评估框架（EEA），通过动作熵、轨迹熵、工具熵、信息增益、探索效率和鲁棒性熵六项指标，从决策过程的结构而非仅从任务完成结果来评估智能体行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统评估指标（任务成功率、奖励、延迟、成本）忽略了Agent行为的关键方面：是否过度探索、是否机械重复、工具使用是否高效、是否随时间减少不确定性、跨多次运行的鲁棒性如何。需要一种补充性框架来捕捉这些被遗漏的行为维度。

Method: 提出EEA（Entropy-Based Evaluation）框架，引入六项基于信息论的指标：动作熵（action entropy）衡量动作选择的随机性/确定性；轨迹熵（trajectory entropy）衡量行为路径的多样性；工具熵（tool entropy）衡量工具使用的分布模式；信息增益（information gain）衡量不确定性的减少程度；探索效率（exploration efficiency）衡量探索行为与收益的关系；鲁棒性熵（robustness entropy）衡量多次运行结果的一致性。框架以轻量级Python实现，可集成LangChain、Google ADK、自定义Agent循环及存储的可观测性轨迹。

Result: EEA框架能够从决策过程的结构性角度揭示传统指标无法捕捉的Agent行为特征，提供了与传统评估方法互补的多维度行为度量，且实现轻量、易于集成到主流Agent开发框架中。

Conclusion: EEA框架通过信息论视角为AI Agent评估提供了新的补充维度，关注决策过程而非仅关注最终结果，有助于更全面、更细致地理解和改进Agent行为。该框架定位为传统评估的补充而非替代，并已提供实用的工程实现。

Abstract: AI agents are commonly evaluated using task success, reward, latency, and cost. These metrics are useful, but they often miss important aspects of agent behavior: whether an agent explores too much, repeats itself too rigidly, uses tools effectively, reduces uncertainty over time, or remains robust across repeated runs. This paper proposes Entropy-Based Evaluation of AI Agents (EEA), a lightweight framework for measuring agent behavior through entropy. Rather than treating intelligence as only final task completion, EEA studies the structure of the agents decision process. The framework introduces action entropy, trajectory entropy, tool entropy, information gain, exploration efficiency, and robustness entropy. These metrics are intended to complement, not replace, traditional evaluation methods. We also present a practical Python implementation designed to integrate with agent frameworks such as LangChain, Google ADK, custom agent loops, and stored observability traces.

</details>


### [353] [Stability vs. Manipulability: Evaluating Robustness Under Post-Decision Interaction in LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05384)
*Srimonti Dutta,Akshata Kishore Moharir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM-as-judge评估看似稳定，但在事后交互中可被显著操纵。研究发现，通过针对性的挑战对话，可以逆转评估结果，降低与人类偏好的一致性，改变基准排名，并产生有害的评估变化。权威框架尤其具有破坏性。作者提出评估鲁棒性得分（ERS）来量化这种交互脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM-as-judge评估范式假设评估结果是固定输入的稳定属性，但忽视了在交互场景下评估可能被操纵的风险。作者旨在揭示这种"决策后可操纵性"的存在及其实际危害，推动评估协议从静态一致性测量转向考虑交互鲁棒性的更全面评估。

Method: 在MT-Bench和AlpacaEval两个基准上设计受控实验：(1) 重复中性重评估测试稳定性；(2) 反基线挑战协议测试针对性推翻判断；(3) 平衡目标验证协议分离可逆性与净方向性引导效应。引入"权威框架"等交互策略，分析判断反转后的理由重叠度，并提出评估鲁棒性得分(ERS)作为综合度量指标。

Result: LLM评委在重复中性重评估下高度稳定，但在针对性事后挑战下显著可逆。权威框架尤其具有破坏性。修订后的判断常伴随低重叠的理由，暗示事后合理化而非真正的纠错。可逆转性导致与人类偏好的对齐度下降、基准排名变化以及有害评估变更，即便模型自信度很高。

Conclusion: 事后交互构成LLM-as-judge评估的一个独特失效模式。评估协议不应仅测量静态一致性，还需纳入挑战下的鲁棒性。ERS为量化这种交互鲁棒性提供了可行工具。

Abstract: LLM-as-judge evaluation is widely used in benchmarking pipelines, where model outputs are compared and ranked using automated evaluators. These pipelines typically assume that judgments are stable properties of fixed inputs. We show that this assumption does not hold under interaction. We study post-decision manipulability: the extent to which an evaluation outcome can be altered through subsequent conversation with the judge after an initial decision has been made. Across controlled experiments on MT-Bench and AlpacaEval, we find that LLM judges are highly stable under repeated and neutral reevaluation, yet become substantially reversible under targeted post-decision challenge. An anti-baseline challenge protocol shows that stable judgments can be overturned through motivated interaction, while a counterbalanced target-validation protocol separates this reversibility from net target-directed steering. These reversals have practical consequences: they can degrade agreement with human preferences, shift benchmark rankings, and produce harmful evaluation changes despite high self-reported confidence. Authority framing is especially destabilizing, and revised judgments are often accompanied by low-overlap justifications, suggesting post hoc rationalization rather than reliable error correction. We introduce the Evaluation Robustness Score (ERS) to quantify interactional robustness by combining reversal susceptibility with counterbalanced directional effects. Our findings identify post-decision interaction as a distinct failure mode for LLM-as-judge evaluation and motivate evaluation protocols that measure not only static agreement, but robustness under challenge.

</details>


### [354] [Learning Visual Spatial Planning from Symbolic State via Modality-Gap-Aware Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06076)
*Haocheng Luo,Jiahui Liu,Ruicheng Zhang,Zhizhou Zhong,Jiaqi Huang,Zunnan Xu,Quan Shi,Jun Zhou,Xiu Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MGSD提出一种两阶段模态差异感知的自蒸馏框架，通过冷启动定位和特权教师策略蒸馏，在不依赖推理阶段符号数据的前提下，显著缩小视觉空间规划与符号规划之间的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在视觉空间规划任务上表现不佳，根源在于感知-推理模态差异（perception-reasoning modality gap）：视觉规划需从像素中推断隐式状态结构再进行推理，形成视觉状态恢复和多步规划的双重瓶颈，而符号规划可直接利用显式对象和约束。

Method: 提出MGSD两阶段框架：(1) 冷启动定位阶段，为视觉学生模型提供可靠的状态表征，减少早期感知噪声；(2) 特权教师蒸馏阶段，利用显式符号状态通过on-policy蒸馏监督学生模型自身的视觉rollout前缀，符号数据仅在训练中使用，推理阶段保持纯视觉输入。

Result: 在视觉规划基准测试上，MGSD在4B和8B backbone上分别将宏平均性能提升19.3%和18.4%，有效缩小与符号输入上限的差距。消融实验和诊断分析证实，提升同时来源于视觉状态恢复和最优路径推理两方面。

Conclusion: 模态差异感知的自蒸馏不仅改善了模型感知可操作状态的能力，也增强了其对推断结构进行规划的能力，为视觉空间规划的感知-推理协同优化提供了有效路径。

Abstract: While vision-language models excel at general multimodal understanding, they still struggle with visual spatial planning. We attribute this to a perception-reasoning modality gap: visual planning requires models to infer latent state structures from pixels and then reason over the recovered structure to produce valid actions, whereas symbolic planning directly leverages explicit objects and constraints. This creates dual bottlenecks in visual state recovery and multi-step planning. To address this, we propose MGSD, a two-stage modality-gap-aware self-distillation framework. First, a cold-start grounding stage equips the visual student with reliable state representations, minimizing early perception noise. Second, a privileged teacher transfers planning capabilities via on-policy distillation, using explicit symbolic states to supervise the student's own visual rollout prefixes. Crucially, symbolic data is used strictly during training, leaving inference purely visual. Experiments on visual planning benchmarks show that MGSD consistently improves visual planning across both 4B and 8B backbones, raising the macro average by 19.3% and 18.4%, respectively. The resulting models narrow the gap to symbolic-input upper bounds, while ablations and diagnostics confirm that the improvement comes from both visual state recovery and optimal-path reasoning. These results suggest that modality-gap-aware self-distillation improves not only how models perceive actionable states, but also how they plan over the inferred structure. Code is available at https://github.com/Oranger-l/MGSD.

</details>


### [355] [LeanMarathon: Toward Reliable AI Co-Mathematicians through Long-Horizon Lean Autoformalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05400)
*Yuanhe Zhang,Yuekai Sun,Taiji Suzuki,Jason D. Lee,Fanghui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LeanMarathon 是一个用于研究级 Lean 自动形式化的多智能体框架，核心是演进式蓝图（blueprint），通过四类智能体在两级编排下协同工作，将脆弱的长时间运行转化为可恢复的并行事务，在四个 Erdős 问题上完成了全部 7 个目标定理的无 sorry 形式化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程自动形式化面临三大挑战：(1) 陈述漂移——形式化过程中语义逐渐偏离原始目标；(2) 依赖纠缠——证明步骤间依赖关系混乱；(3) 上下文衰减——局部修复破坏远端已有工作。现有方法在规模上失效，需要一个能保持目标保真度的持久化框架。

Method: LeanMarathon 的核心是"演进式蓝图"——一个 Lean 文件，同时充当形式化证明骨架、自然语言证明图和共享系统记录。四类按合约划分的智能体（构建、审计、证明、修复）围绕蓝图工作。两级编排器：第一级通过对抗性审查稳定目标保真度；第二级从动态叶节点向上、以并行 CI 门控轮次逐层释放证明 DAG。

Result: 在涵盖四个 Erdős 问题（#1051, #1196, #164, #1217）的两篇近期研究论文上评测，三次独立运行中，LeanMarathon 形式化了全部 7 个目标定理，无任何 sorry，共证明 258 条引理和定理。

Conclusion: 可靠的 AI 协同数学不仅需要更强的证明器，还需要能在长程数学推导中保持目标保真度的持久化框架。LeanMarathon 证明了这种架构的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Long-horizon autoformalization of research mathematics fails not only at hard lemmas, but at scale: statements drift, dependencies tangle, context decays, and local repairs corrupt distant work. We present LeanMarathon, a multi-agent harness for reliable research-level Lean autoformalization. Its core abstraction is an evolving blueprint: a Lean file that serves simultaneously as formal proof skeleton, natural-language proof graph, and shared system of record. Four contract-scoped agents construct, audit, prove, and repair this blueprint. These agents are coordinated by a two-stage orchestrator that first stabilizes target fidelity through adversarial review and then discharges the proof directed acyclic graph (DAG) from its dynamic leaves upward in parallel CI-gated rounds. LeanMarathon turns one brittle multi-hour run into many local, recoverable, parallel transactions. We evaluate LeanMarathon on two recent research papers spanning four Erdős problems (#1051, #1196, #164, #1217). Across three autonomous runs, it formalizes all seven target theorems with no sorry, proving 258 lemmas and theorems. These results show that reliable AI co-mathematics requires not only stronger provers, but durable harnesses that preserve target fidelity across long mathematical developments. The code can be found at https://github.com/YuanheZ/LeanMarathon.

</details>


### [356] [Harnessing Generalist Agents for Contextualized Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05404)
*Zihao Li,Kaifeng Jin,Yuanchen Bei,Jiaru Zou,Avaneesh Kumar,Xuying Ning,Yanjun Zhao,Mengting Ai,Baoyu Jing,Hanghang Tong,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TimeClaw 是一个面向时间序列的代理框架，通过集成可执行时间工具、经验驱动的能力演化和多模态情景记忆，使通用大语言模型具备上下文感知的时间推理能力，在多个真实领域基准上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列数据通常嵌入在丰富的上下文环境中，但现有通用AI代理主要在文本空间运行，与结构化时间信号不匹配；同时，实际应用需要端到端分析流程，而预测等单一任务只是其中的一环。

Method: 提出TimeClaw框架，包含三大核心组件：(1) 可执行时间工具，用于有据可查、可审计的分析；(2) 经验驱动的能力演化机制，用于创建可复用的分析例程；(3) 情景化多模态记忆，用于检索相关推理轨迹。

Result: 在能源、金融、天气、交通等多个真实世界领域的多样化任务基准上进行了广泛评估，结果表明TimeClaw显著提升了时间序列分析的性能。

Conclusion: TimeClaw通过为通用LLM代理配备时间序列原生运行时支持，成功解锁了融合上下文信息的开放式时间推理能力，为构建端到端时间序列分析工作流提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Time series are often embedded in rich contexts that are essential for holistic modeling. Moreover, real-world practitioners often require end-to-end workflows for analyzing temporal dynamics, where widely studied tasks such as forecasting are only one step in a broader solution loop. While generalist AI agents offer a promising interface for such workflows under complex contexts, they still operate primarily in textual spaces that are not fully aligned with structured temporal signals. In this work, we introduce TimeClaw, an agentic harness framework for time series that equips generalist LLM agents with the time series-native runtime support needed for contextualized temporal reasoning. TimeClaw integrates executable temporal tools for grounded and auditable analysis, experience-driven capability evolution for creating reusable analytical routines, and episodic multimodal memory for retrieving relevant reasoning traces. Together, these components unlock harnessed open-ended temporal reasoning with contextual information. Extensive evaluation on multiple benchmarks covering diverse tasks across energy, finance, weather, traffic, and other real-world domains demonstrates improved performance of TimeClaw. Code is available at https://github.com/iDEA-iSAIL-Lab-UIUC/TimeClaw.

</details>


### [357] [Agents' Last Exam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05405)
*Yiyou Sun,Xinyang Han,Weichen Zhang,Yuanbo Pang,Tianyu Wang,Yuhan Cao,Yixiao Huang,Chris Duroiu,Haoyun Zhang,Jeffrey Lin,Weishu Zhang,Tyler Zeng,Ying Yan,Bo Liu,Hanson Wen,Mingyang Xu,Xiaoyuan Liu,Zimeng Chen,Weiyan Shi,Amanda Dsouza,Vincent Sunn Chen,Patrick Bryant,Carl Boettiger,Yamini Rangan,Bradley Rothenberg,Kyle Steinfeld,Arvind Rao,Tapio Schneider,Georgios Yannakakis,Laure Zanna,Kaan
